  • 世卫组织已发布有关 H5N1 病毒的警报,由于印度同时与 季节性流感 A(H1N1) 和禽流感 暴发 作斗争,该病毒变得越来越紧迫。该警报是基于 H5N1 在鸟类中的持续传播以及动物向人类 跨物种溢出 的可能性。
  • 香港推出了 2026-27 年 季节性流感 疫苗接种 计划,比上一年提前一周开始。政府对 疫苗 采购进行了多元化,并简化了行政措施,以促进早期 疫苗接种 的实施。该事件的确认基于官方声明和政策变化。

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-28  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.00662-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0066226

低占用率的 N 连接糖基化位点支持 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒在人群中持续循环。

N-linked glycosylation sites with low occupancy support sustained circulation of the A(H3N2) influenza A virus in the human population.
作者： Irina V. Alymova, Betlehem Mekonnen, Dongxia Wang, Ram P. Kamal, Wen-Pin Tzeng, Alexander S. Jureka, John R. Barr, Shane Gansebom, Ian A. York
摘要中文翻译摘要 甲型流感病毒血凝素的糖基化对于病毒适应性和免疫逃避至关重要。当代人类 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒的血凝素被广泛糖基化,具有多达 13 个假定的糖基化位点。这些位点的聚糖类型和占用情况并不统一:虽然大多数几乎完全被聚糖占据,但有些位点(例如氨基酸残基 45 和 144 处的聚糖)仅被部分占据,并且在进化上不稳定。虽然血凝素高度占据的糖基化位点对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒生物学的影响已得到充分研究,但有关低占据糖基化位点对病毒血凝素功能影响的信息有限。在这里,通过使用血凝素中残基 45 或 144 或两者都缺乏聚糖的反向遗传学 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒,我们证明,尽管受体结合、热稳定性和融合降低,但这些位置低水平糖基化的存在不会影响病毒在 Madin-Darby 犬肾上皮细胞培养物中的生长。相比之下,这些低占用位点确实通过降低与病毒中和和预防流感疾病相关的抗体滴度来影响小鼠的 免疫应答。我们建议,暂时引入低糖基化位点可以使流感病毒减少对血凝素抗原和受体结合位点的免疫压力,而不会显着损害病毒毒力。这种机制可能有助于支持 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒在人群中的持续传播。重要性 甲型流感病毒血凝素上的许多糖基化位点靠近抗原位点,因此聚糖会阻止抗体识别并帮助逃避群体免疫。然而,聚糖也可能降低病毒复制和毒力。当代 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒血凝素氨基酸残基 45 和 144 处的糖基化位点被最小化,只有不到 10% 的位点含有聚糖。这仍然足以部分逃避基于抗体的群体免疫,同时最大限度地减少对复制和毒力的影响。随着时间的推移,甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒仅在有限的时间内使用这两个位点,这表明即使聚糖占据水平较低也可能会带来一些负选择压力,而这种压力可以通过逃避人类群体免疫力来补偿。这项研究的数据让我们更好地了解病毒逃避基于抗体的免疫并维持在人群中循环的机制。然而,聚糖也可能降低病毒复制和毒力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究低占有率糖基化位点对 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒血凝素的功能影响及其在免疫逃避和病毒持久性中的作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及反向遗传学来生成在特定血凝素残基处缺乏聚糖的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒,并在 Madin-Darby 犬肾上皮细胞培养物和小鼠中测试了这些病毒。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学创建了在残基 45 和 144 处糖基化位点经过修饰的 甲型H3N2流感病毒 甲型流感病毒,并评估了它们对细胞培养物中病毒生长和小鼠体内 免疫应答 的影响。
主要结果
残基 45 和 144 处存在低糖基化并不影响细胞培养物中的病毒生长,但它降低了小鼠体内与病毒中和和预防流感疾病相关的抗体滴度。
解释与创新
作者认为,低占有率糖基化位点可能有助于流感病毒减少关键抗原位点的免疫压力,而不会显着损害毒力,从而为病毒免疫逃避策略提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能通过强调低占用糖基化位点在维持人群中病毒循环和免疫逃避中的作用,为 疫苗 设计和 监测 的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,证据中等,因为它不包括长期人群水平数据或临床结果。

N-linked glycosylation sites with low occupancy support sustained circulation of the A(H3N2) influenza A virus in the human population.

Authors: Irina V. Alymova, Betlehem Mekonnen, Dongxia Wang, Ram P. Kamal, Wen-Pin Tzeng, Alexander S. Jureka, John R. Barr, Shane Gansebom, Ian A. York
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Glycosylation of the influenza A virus hemagglutinin is crucial for viral fitness and immune evasion. The hemagglutinin of contemporary human A(H3N2) influenza A viruses is extensively glycosylated with up to 13 putative glycosylation sites. Glycan types and occupancy of these sites are not uniform: while most are nearly completely occupied by glycans, some, such as those at amino acid residues 45 and 144, are only partially occupied and are evolutionarily unstable. While the effects of highly occupied glycosylated sites of hemagglutinin on A(H3N2) influenza A virus biology are well studied, there is limited information on the functional impact of the low-occupancy glycosylation sites on the viral hemagglutinin. Here, by using reverse-genetics A(H3N2) influenza A viruses lacking glycans at either residue 45 or 144, or both in hemagglutinin, we demonstrate that, despite reduced receptor binding, thermal stability, and fusion, the presence of a low level of glycosylation at these positions does not impact virus growth in Madin-Darby canine kidney epithelial cell culture. In contrast, these low-occupancy sites do affect immune responses in mice, by reducing titers of antibodies that correlate with virus neutralization and protection against influenza disease. We suggest that the temporary introduction of sites with low glycosylation allows influenza viruses to reduce the immune pressure on antigenic and receptor binding sites of hemagglutinin without significantly compromising viral virulence. This mechanism may help support the sustained circulation of A(H3N2) influenza A virus in human populations. IMPORTANCE Many of the glycosylation sites on the hemagglutinin of influenza A viruses are near antigenic sites, such that the glycans block antibody recognition and help evade population immunity. However, glycans may also reduce viral replication and virulence. Glycosylation sites at amino acid residues 45 and 144 of the hemagglutinin of contemporary A(H3N2) influenza A viruses are minimally occupied, with fewer than 10% of the sites containing glycans. This is still sufficient to partially evade antibody-based population immunity, while minimizing the impact on replication and virulence. Over time, A(H3N2) viruses have used both these sites only during limited periods, suggesting that even a low level of glycan occupancy may confer some negative selection pressure that is compensated by evasion of human population immunity. Data from this study provide a better understanding of the mechanisms that allow viruses to evade antibody-based immunity and maintain circulation in the human population. However, glycans may also reduce viral replication and virulence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the functional impact of low-occupancy glycosylation sites on the hemagglutinin of A(H3N2) influenza A viruses and their role in immune evasion and viral persistence.
Design and population
The study involved reverse-genetics to generate A(H3N2) influenza A viruses lacking glycans at specific hemagglutinin residues, and tested these viruses in Madin-Darby canine kidney epithelial cell culture and in mice.
Core methods
The researchers used reverse-genetics to create A(H3N2) influenza A viruses with modified glycosylation sites at residues 45 and 144, and assessed their effects on viral growth in cell culture and immune responses in mice.
Main results
The presence of low glycosylation at residues 45 and 144 did not affect virus growth in cell culture, but it reduced antibody titers in mice that correlate with virus neutralization and protection against influenza disease.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that low-occupancy glycosylation sites may help influenza viruses reduce immune pressure on key antigenic sites without significantly compromising virulence, offering a novel insight into viral immune evasion strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform strategies for vaccine design and surveillance by highlighting the role of low-occupancy glycosylation sites in maintaining viral circulation and immune evasion in human populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, and the evidence is moderate as it does not include long-term population-level data or clinical outcomes.

重复接种疫苗的个体的记忆 B 细胞库包括流感血凝素特异性跨群结合物,其亲和力超出 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 季节性菌株

Memory B Cell Repertoire of a Repeatedly Vaccinated Individual Includes Influenza Hemagglutinin-Specific Cross-Group Binders with Affinity Beyond A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H3N2) Seasonal Strains
作者： Margarita Mishina, Christopher M. Horn, Zachary Ende, Vasiliy Mishin, Crystal Holiday, Sujatha Seenu, Jason R. Wilson, Weiping Cao, Mili Sheth, Dhwani Batra
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:人类主要感染两种不同的 季节性流感 A 病毒 (IAV) 亚型:甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒。由于 IAV 突变迅速,它会不断逃避现有的保护性抗体。然而,反复接触这些不断进化的毒株会增加病毒特异性记忆 B 细胞 (MBC) 群体的多样性。为了调查成人 IAV 特异性免疫的多样性和交叉反应性程度,及其对新出现菌株的可能影响,我们分析了经常接受季节性 疫苗 免疫的成人的血凝素 (HA) 特异性 MBC 库。方法:对从重复 季节性流感 疫苗接种 的个体收集的 HA 探针阳性 MBC 进行高通量单细胞测序。测试了来自多细胞克隆型的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的 HA 结合和流感病毒中和作用。结果:鉴定出两种克隆型,它们与采血季前后循环菌株中的 A(H1) 和 A(H3) HA 结合。两种克隆型均中和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 菌株,而其中一种也中和 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 菌株。值得注意的是,这些跨基团结合剂表现出对 A(H5)、A(H7) 和 A(H9) 禽流感 HA 以及中和牛 A(H5N1) IAV 的亲和力。结论:我们的研究结果表明,季节性 疫苗接种 可以支持某些个体预先存在的交叉反应 B 细胞免疫。这强调了研究不同人群的流感特异性 MBC 库的重要性,以更好地了解免疫动态并改进 疫苗 设计。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查重复季节性 疫苗接种 的成人甲型流感病毒特异性记忆 B 细胞 (MBC) 免疫的多样性和交叉反应性程度,解决快速病毒 突变 和免疫逃逸的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究分析了重复 季节性流感 疫苗接种 的成人的血凝素 (HA) 特异性 MBC 库,重点关注这种免疫对新兴菌株的影响。
核心方法
对 HA 探针阳性 MBC 进行高通量单细胞测序,并测试来自多细胞克隆型的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的 HA 结合和流感病毒中和作用。
主要结果
鉴定出两种克隆型,它们与采血季前后循环菌株中的 A(H1) 和 A(H3) HA 结合。两种克隆型均中和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 菌株,而其中一种也中和 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 菌株。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,重复季节性 疫苗接种 可以支持某些个体中预先存在的交叉反应性 B 细胞免疫,凸显了针对流感亚型提供广泛保护的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解免疫动力学,并为改进 疫苗 设计以增强 季节性流感 的交叉菌株保护提供见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个个体,这可能会影响普遍性,并且证据基于单个案例,导致置信度中等。

Memory B Cell Repertoire of a Repeatedly Vaccinated Individual Includes Influenza Hemagglutinin-Specific Cross-Group Binders with Affinity Beyond A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H3N2) Seasonal Strains

Authors: Margarita Mishina, Christopher M. Horn, Zachary Ende, Vasiliy Mishin, Crystal Holiday, Sujatha Seenu, Jason R. Wilson, Weiping Cao, Mili Sheth, Dhwani Batra
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Humans are primarily infected by two distinct seasonal influenza A virus (IAV) subtypes, A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H3N2). Because IAV mutates rapidly, it continuously escapes existing protective antibodies. However, repeated exposure to these evolving strains increases the diversity of virus-specific memory B cell (MBC) population. To investigate the extent of this diversity and cross-reactivity of IAV- specific immunity in adults, and its possible impact on emerging strains, we analyzed the hemagglutinin (HA)-specific MBC repertoire of an adult frequently immunized with seasonal vaccines. Methods: High-throughput single-cell sequencing of HA probe-positive MBCs collected from an individual with repeated seasonal influenza vaccination was performed. Monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) from multicell clonotypes were tested for HA binding and influenza virus neutralization. Results: Two clonotypes were identified that bound to both A(H1) and A(H3) HAs from strains circulating before and after the season of blood collection. Both clonotypes neutralized A(H3N2) strains, while one also neutralized A(H1N1)pdm09 strains. Notably, these cross-group binders showed affinity for A(H5), A(H7) and A(H9) avian influenza HAs and neutralized bovine A(H5N1) IAV. Conclusions: Our findings suggest that seasonal vaccination can support pre-existing cross-reactive B-cell immunity in some individuals. This underscores the importance of studying influenza-specific MBC repertoires across diverse populations to better understand immune dynamics and improve vaccine design.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the extent of diversity and cross-reactivity of influenza A virus-specific memory B cell (MBC) immunity in adults with repeated seasonal vaccination, addressing the challenge of rapid viral mutation and immune escape.
Design and population
The study analyzed the hemagglutinin (HA)-specific MBC repertoire of an adult with repeated seasonal influenza vaccination, focusing on the impact of this immunity on emerging strains.
Core methods
High-throughput single-cell sequencing of HA probe-positive MBCs was conducted, and monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) from multicell clonotypes were tested for HA binding and influenza virus neutralization.
Main results
Two clonotypes were identified that bound to both A(H1) and A(H3) HAs from strains circulating before and after the season of blood collection. Both clonotypes neutralized A(H3N2) strains, while one also neutralized A(H1N1)pdm09 strains.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that repeated seasonal vaccination can support pre-existing cross-reactive B-cell immunity in some individuals, highlighting the potential for broad protection against influenza subtypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform the understanding of immune dynamics and provide insights for improving vaccine design to enhance cross-strain protection in seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single individual, which may affect generalizability, and the evidence is based on a single case, resulting in moderate confidence.

甲型流感病毒 H5N1 基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 在源自上呼吸道和下呼吸道的原代人呼吸道上皮细胞培养物中表现出温度依赖性复制限制。

Influenza A virus H5N1 genotypes B3.13 and D1.1 show temperature-dependent restriction of replication in primary human respiratory epithelial cell cultures derived from the upper and lower respiratory tract.
作者： Anne P. Werner, Jaiprasath Sachithanandham, Elgin Akin, Sattya Talukdar, Matthew Pinsley, Andrew Pekosz, Werner, A. P.
摘要中文翻译H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 甲型禽流感病毒对野生动物种群、家养动物以及潜在的人类种群构成重大威胁。为了使 H5N1 在哺乳动物物种之间感染和传播,需要获得 突变 来提高哺乳动物受体的利用率并增强上呼吸道较低温度下的复制。将人类 H1N1pdm09 样病毒与 H5N1 基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 进行比较,以在 33°C、37°C 和 39°C 下复制,这些温度与人类上呼吸道和下呼吸道以及奶牛乳房组织一致。与 H1N1pdm09 相比,所有 H5N1 病毒在 37{o}C 和 39{o}C 下 MDCK 细胞上的噬斑大小均有所增加。在原代、分化的人类鼻腔和支气管上皮培养物中,与 H1N1 相比,所有 H5N1 病毒在 33°C 下的感染性病毒产量均受到限制。虽然 H5N1 D1.1 在 37{o}C 和 39{o}C 下也表现出有限的复制,但 H5N1 B3.13 的复制滴度几乎与 H1N1pdm09 相同。所有 H5N1 病毒在上呼吸道和下呼吸道细胞中都表现出相似的细胞趋向性,相对于 H1N1pdm09,它们感染的纤毛细胞多于非纤毛细胞。H1N1、H5N1 B3.13 D1.1 感染诱导类似的先天免疫因子,鼻上皮细胞产生的水平高于支气管上皮细胞。这些数据表明,与 H1N1pdm09 相比,基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 H5N1 病毒表现出不同的温度依赖性复制模式。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 H5N1 基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 在人类呼吸道上皮细胞培养物中的复制特征,解决有关它们在哺乳动物物种之间感染和传播的潜力的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究比较了具有 H5N1 基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 的人类 H1N1pdm09 样病毒在源自上呼吸道和下呼吸道的原代人呼吸道上皮细胞培养物中的复制情况。
核心方法
研究人员通过测量不同温度(33°C、37°C​​ 和 39°C)下 MDCK 细胞上的噬斑大小来评估复制,并评估分化的人鼻和支气管上皮培养物中感染性病毒的产生。
主要结果
与 H1N1pdm09 相比,所有 H5N1 病毒在 37°C 和 39°C 的 MDCK 细胞上显示出噬斑大小增加。在人呼吸道上皮培养物中,H5N1 B3.13 在 33°C 时复制到几乎与 H1N1pdm09 相同的滴度,而 H5N1 D1.1 在 37°C 和 39°C 时表现出有限的复制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,与 H1N1pdm09 相比,H5N1 基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 表现出明显的温度依赖性复制模式,这可能对其在哺乳动物 宿主 中感染和传播的潜力产生影响。
科研与公卫意义
结果提供了对 H5N1 病毒在人类呼吸道组织中的复制行为的深入了解,这可以为 监测 和潜在人畜共患 传播 的风险评估策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外细胞培养,并未考虑可能影响复制动力学的体内因素。由于受控实验设置,证据是中等的。

Influenza A virus H5N1 genotypes B3.13 and D1.1 show temperature-dependent restriction of replication in primary human respiratory epithelial cell cultures derived from the upper and lower respiratory tract.

Authors: Anne P. Werner, Jaiprasath Sachithanandham, Elgin Akin, Sattya Talukdar, Matthew Pinsley, Andrew Pekosz, Werner, A. P.
Original abstract (English)
H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b avian influenza A viruses pose a significant threat to wild animal populations, domesticated animals, and potentially, the human population. For H5N1s to infect and transmit among mammalian species, mutations for improved utilization of mammalian receptors and enhanced replication at the lower temperatures of the upper respiratory tract need to be acquired. A human H1N1pdm09-like virus was compared to H5N1 genotypes B3.13 and D1.1 for replication at 33{o}C, 37{o}C, and 39{o}C - temperatures consistent with the upper and lower respiratory tract in humans, and dairy cow udder tissue. All H5N1 viruses had increased plaque sizes on MDCK cells at 37{o}C and 39{o}C compared to H1N1pdm09. In primary, differentiated human nasal and bronchial epithelial cultures, all H5N1 viruses show restricted infectious virus production compared to H1N1 at 33{o}C. While H5N1 D1.1 also showed restricted replication at 37{o}C and 39{o}C, the H5N1 B3.13 replicated to nearly equivalent titers as H1N1pdm09. All H5N1 viruses demonstrated similar cell tropism in cells from the upper and lower respiratory tract, infecting more ciliated than non-ciliated cells relative to H1N1pdm09. H1N1, H5N1 B3.13 D1.1 infection induced similar innate immune factors, with nasal epithelial cells producing higher levels compared to bronchial epithelial cells. These data suggest that genotype B3.13 and D1.1 H5N1 viruses show different temperature dependent replication patterns compared to H1N1pdm09.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the replication characteristics of H5N1 genotypes B3.13 and D1.1 in human respiratory epithelial cell cultures, addressing the knowledge gap about their potential to infect and transmit among mammalian species.
Design and population
The study compared the replication of a human H1N1pdm09-like virus with H5N1 genotypes B3.13 and D1.1 in primary human respiratory epithelial cell cultures derived from both upper and lower respiratory tracts.
Core methods
The researchers assessed replication by measuring plaque sizes on MDCK cells at different temperatures (33°C, 37°C, and 39°C) and evaluated infectious virus production in differentiated human nasal and bronchial epithelial cultures.
Main results
All H5N1 viruses showed increased plaque sizes on MDCK cells at 37°C and 39°C compared to H1N1pdm09. In human respiratory epithelial cultures, H5N1 B3.13 replicated to nearly equivalent titers as H1N1pdm09 at 33°C, while H5N1 D1.1 showed restricted replication at 37°C and 39°C.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that H5N1 genotypes B3.13 and D1.1 exhibit distinct temperature-dependent replication patterns compared to H1N1pdm09, which may have implications for their potential to infect and transmit among mammalian hosts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the replication behavior of H5N1 viruses in human respiratory tissues, which could inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for potential zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro cell cultures and does not account for in vivo factors, which may affect replication dynamics. The evidence is moderate due to the controlled experimental setting.

法学硕士能够根据社交媒体数据实时估计 季节性流感 疫苗 的有效性

An LLM enabled real-time estimation of seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness from social media data
作者： Michael J Pavia, Ivan Flores Amaro, Dongfang Xu, Graciela Gonzalez-Hernandez, Matthew Scotch, Pavia, M. J., Amaro, I. F.
摘要中文翻译流感 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 是根据有限数量的诊所使用测试阴性设计估算得出的。这些标准估计面临地理、时间和操作方面的限制。使用 Twitter/X 数据,我们应用了几次思维链提示来识别自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态和流感测试结果,然后实施类似测试阴性的设计来估计 VE。我们的估计属于中期报告的范围,可以补充当前系统,提高可行性、及时性和可扩展性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过探索替代数据源来解决估计 季节性流感 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 的传统方法的局限性。作者强调了当前 VE 估计方法的地理、时间和操作限制。
设计与对象
该研究是使用 Twitter/X 数据进行的,重点关注人群中的 季节性流感 病毒。它涉及应用类似测试阴性的设计,根据自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态和流感测试结果来估计 VE。
核心方法
研究人员使用法学硕士的少量思维链提示,从 Twitter/X 数据中识别自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态和流感检测结果。然后,他们实施了类似测试阴性的设计来估计 疫苗 的有效性。
主要结果
Twitter/X数据推导出的估计值落在中期报告的范围内,表明该方法可作为当前疫苗有效性(VE)估计系统的补充。
解释与创新
作者将其研究结果解释为证明了利用社交媒体数据估计疫苗有效性的可行性,并提出了一种可提高及时性和可扩展性的新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将社交媒体数据整合到 VE 估计中可以增强 监测 系统,为公共卫生决策提供更及时和可扩展的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖社交媒体的自我报告数据可能会引入偏差,而且结果的普遍性有限。然而,考虑到结果与中期报告的一致性,证据是强有力的。

An LLM enabled real-time estimation of seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness from social media data

Authors: Michael J Pavia, Ivan Flores Amaro, Dongfang Xu, Graciela Gonzalez-Hernandez, Matthew Scotch, Pavia, M. J., Amaro, I. F.
Original abstract (English)
Influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) is estimated from a limited number of clinics using a test-negative design. These standard estimates face geographic, temporal, and operational constraints. Using Twitter/X data, we applied few-shot chain-of-thought prompting to identify self-reported vaccination status and influenza test results, then implemented a test-negative-like design to estimate VE. Our estimates fell within the range of interim reports and could complement current systems, improving feasibility, timeliness, and scalability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional methods for estimating seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness (VE) by exploring alternative data sources. The authors highlighted the geographic, temporal, and operational constraints of current VE estimation approaches.
Design and population
The study was conducted using Twitter/X data, focusing on seasonal influenza viruses in human populations. It involved applying a test-negative-like design to estimate VE based on self-reported vaccination status and influenza test results.
Core methods
The researchers used few-shot chain-of-thought prompting with an LLM to identify self-reported vaccination status and influenza test results from Twitter/X data. They then implemented a test-negative-like design to estimate vaccine effectiveness.
Main results
The estimates derived from the Twitter/X data fell within the range of interim reports, indicating that the method could complement current systems for VE estimation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the feasibility of using social media data to estimate seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness, offering a novel approach that could improve timeliness and scalability.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating social media data into VE estimation could enhance surveillance systems, providing more timely and scalable insights for public health decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on self-reported data from social media may introduce biases, and the generalizability of the results is limited. However, the evidence is strong given the alignment of results with interim reports.

A/H1N1、A/H3N2 和 Flu-B 感染后 GSDMD 和 TfR-1 基因显着上调

Significant upregulation of GSDMD and TfR-1 genes following A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and Flu-B infections
作者： Arash Letafati, Simin Abbasi, Talat Mokhtari Azad, Jila Yavarian
摘要中文翻译流感病毒作为季节性流感的重要呼吸道病毒,是导致人群每年出现死亡率的原因。在细胞内不同且复杂的相互作用中,细胞焦亡和铁死亡已成为与流感感染后炎症相关的两种主要细胞死亡途径。在这项研究中,我们研究了感染不同类型流感病毒的 A549 感染细胞中 48 小时感染后 (hpi) 的 GSDMD(细胞焦亡标记)和 TfR-1(铁死亡标记)mRNA 表达。A549 细胞用 100 TCID50 感染 A/H1N1、A/H3N2 和 Flu-B。使用RNJIA RNA提取试剂盒(RNJIA,ROJE,伊朗)提取48 hpi的RNA,并通过SYBR Green qRT-PCR评估GSDMD和TfR-1的mRNA表达。使用非参数和参数统计检验分析基因表达数据,然后进行成对事后比较。与对照组相比,GSDMD 和 TfR-1 在 A/H1N1、A/H3N2 和 Flu-B 中持续上调,表明这些促焦亡和促铁死亡因子与炎症后 48 hpi 后的细胞焦亡和铁死亡途径相关。与 A/H3N2 和 Flu-B 相比,GSDMD 在 A/H1N1 中上调最明显,但 TfR-1 上调表现出不同的模式,在 A/H3N2 中上调最高。总的来说,我们的研究支持 GSDMD 和 TfR-1 的潜在参与,它们与不同类型流感病毒后活跃的细胞焦亡和铁死亡相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感病毒是重要的呼吸道病原体,导致人群每年出现 死亡率。本研究旨在探讨流感感染后 GSDMD 和 TfR-1 基因的表达。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用 A/H1N1、A/H3N2 和 Flu-B 病毒以 100 TCID50 的剂量感染 A549 细胞。该研究的重点是分析感染后 48 小时与对照组相比的基因表达变化。
核心方法
使用 RNJIA RNA 提取试剂盒提取 48 hpi 的 RNA,并使用 SYBR Green qRT-PCR 评估 GSDMD 和 TfR-1 的 mRNA 表达。通过非参数和参数统计检验分析基因表达数据,然后进行成对事后比较。
主要结果
与对照组相比,在 A/H1N1、A/H3N2 和 Flu-B 中均观察到 GSDMD 和 TfR-1 的上调。GSDMD 在 A/H1N1 中表现出最剧烈的上调,而 TfR-1 在 A/H3N2 中表现出最高的上调。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,GSDMD 和 TfR-1 参与不同流感病毒引起的炎症后的细胞焦亡和铁死亡途径。这项研究为与 季节性流感 相关的细胞死亡分子机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来关于细胞焦亡和铁死亡在流感引起的炎症中的作用的研究提供信息,可能有助于开发 季节性流感 的靶向疗法或诊断工具。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于使用 A549 细胞进行体外分析,可能无法完全代表人类 免疫应答。证据是中等的,因为它基于基因表达分析,没有直接的临床结果。

Significant upregulation of GSDMD and TfR-1 genes following A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and Flu-B infections

Authors: Arash Letafati, Simin Abbasi, Talat Mokhtari Azad, Jila Yavarian
Original abstract (English)
Influenza viruses, as important respiratory viruses for seasonal flu, are responsible for yearly mortality among human populations. Among different and complex interactions within the cell, pyroptosis and ferroptosis have emerged as two main cell death pathways that are correlated to inflammation following influenza infection. In this study, we have investigated GSDMD (pyroptosis marker) and TfR-1 (ferroptosis marker) mRNA expression, 48 h post-infection (hpi) in A549 infected cells with different types of influenza viruses. A549 cells were infected with A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and Flu-B with 100 TCID50. RNA was extracted by RNJIA RNA extraction kit (RNJIA, ROJE, Iran) 48 hpi, and mRNA expression of GSDMD and TfR-1 was assessed by SYBR Green qRT-PCR. Gene expression data were analyzed using both non-parametric and parametric statistical tests, followed by pairwise post-hoc comparisons. The consistent upregulation of GSDMD and TfR-1 across A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and Flu-B compared to the control groups suggests the involvement of these pro-pyroptotic and pro-ferroptotic factors associated to pyrotposis and ferroptosis cell death pathways following inflammation, 48 hpi. While GSDMD had sharpest upregulation in A/H1N1 compared to A/H3N2 and Flu-B, TfR-1 upregulation showed different pattern with highest upregulation in A/H3N2. In total, our study supports the potential involvement of GSDMD and TfR-1 which are associated with pyroptosis and ferroptosis that are active following different types of influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza viruses are important respiratory pathogens responsible for yearly mortality in human populations. This study aimed to investigate the expression of GSDMD and TfR-1 genes following influenza infections.
Design and population
The study involved infecting A549 cells with A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and Flu-B viruses at a dose of 100 TCID50. The research focused on analyzing gene expression changes 48 hours post-infection compared to control groups.
Core methods
RNA was extracted using the RNJIA RNA extraction kit 48 hpi, and mRNA expression of GSDMD and TfR-1 was assessed using SYBR Green qRT-PCR. Gene expression data were analyzed with both non-parametric and parametric statistical tests, followed by pairwise post-hoc comparisons.
Main results
The upregulation of GSDMD and TfR-1 was consistently observed across A/H1N1, A/H3N2, and Flu-B compared to control groups. GSDMD showed the sharpest upregulation in A/H1N1, while TfR-1 had the highest upregulation in A/H3N2.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that GSDMD and TfR-1 are involved in pyroptosis and ferroptosis pathways following inflammation caused by different influenza viruses. This study provides novel insights into the molecular mechanisms of cell death associated with seasonal influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform future research on the role of pyroptosis and ferroptosis in influenza-induced inflammation, potentially aiding in the development of targeted therapies or diagnostic tools for seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro analysis using A549 cells, which may not fully represent human immune responses. The evidence is moderate as it is based on gene expression analysis without direct clinical outcomes.

70 岁的马流感病毒:马教会了我们关于流感的知识

Equine influenza virus at 70: What horses taught us about flu
作者： Laura Mojsiejczuk, Thomas M. Chambers, Pablo R. Murcia, Mojsiejczuk L, Chambers TM, Murcia PR.
摘要中文翻译这些发现支持了当时已经在讨论的观点,即流感病毒亚型可能“循环利用”,并且动物 储存宿主 可以在这一过程中发挥作用。这些发现后来也在天然 暴发 [8] 中得到了证实,同样使用克隆测序。正如 Bryan Grenfell 在 2022 年京都基础科学奖纪念演讲中指出的那样,对马流感流行的研究激发了成为系统动力学理论核心的想法 [12]。PLOS 病原体 | https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.ppat.1014551 2026 年 8 月 28 日 1 / 6 开放获取引用:Mojsiejczuk L,Chambers TM,Murcia PR (2026) 70 岁时的马流感病毒:马教会了我们关于流感的知识。PLoS Pathog 22(8):e1014551。https://doi.org/10.1371/journal。ppat.1014551 编辑:Sallie R. Murcia 1* 1 MRC-格拉斯哥大学病毒研究中心、感染、免疫和炎症研究所、医学、兽医和生命科学学院...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了马流感病毒 (EIV) 的研究如何有助于理解流感病毒生物学、进化 和 流行病学 的基本方面,特别是与人类 季节性流感 病毒相关的基础知识。
设计与对象
该研究是一项关于马流感病毒的历史与当代研究的叙述性综述,重点关注其作为理解流感动态的模型系统的作用。研究纳入了来自暴发马匹的数据,并与人流感进行了比较。
核心方法
作者对与马流感病毒相关的文献和历史记录进行了全面综述,分析了其流行病学特征、遗传进化及其与宿主宿主种群的相互作用。他们将马流感病毒与人流感病毒进行了比较,以识别在传播和发病机制方面的相似之处与差异。
主要结果
该研究强调,马流感病毒为流感病毒生态学、进化 和 宿主 相互作用提供了重要见解,特别是由于其独特的流行病学特征类似于人类 季节性流感 病毒。
解释与创新
作者解释说,EIV 是研究流感的一个有价值的模型,为病毒 传播 和 进化 提供了独特的视角。这是了解动物模型如何为人类流感研究提供信息的新颖贡献。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,研究马流感病毒可以增强我们对人类 季节性流感 的了解,有助于开发更有效的 监测、诊断 和人类流感控制策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于历史和观察性数据,可能在证据强度方面存在局限性。然而,作者基于多项已记录研究及与人流感的比较,提供了一份支持充分的叙述性综述。

Equine influenza virus at 70: What horses taught us about flu

Authors: Laura Mojsiejczuk, Thomas M. Chambers, Pablo R. Murcia, Mojsiejczuk L, Chambers TM, Murcia PR.
Original abstract (English)
These findings supported the idea, already under discussion at the time, that influenza virus subtypes may “recycle” and that animal reservoirs could play a role in this process. These findings were later confirmed in natural outbreaks [8], also using clonal sequencing. As noted by Bryan Grenfell during the Commemorative lecture of the 2022 Kyoto Prize in Basic Science, the study of epidemics of influenza in horses stimulated ideas that became central to the theories in phylodynamics [12]. Question and background
This study explores how research on equine influenza virus (EIV) has contributed to understanding fundamental aspects of influenza virus biology, evolution, and epidemiology, particularly in relation to human seasonal influenza viruses.
Design and population
The study is a narrative review of historical and contemporary research on equine influenza virus, focusing on its role as a model system for understanding influenza dynamics. It includes data from outbreaks in horses and comparisons with human influenza.
Core methods
The authors conducted a comprehensive review of literature and historical records related to equine influenza virus, analyzing its epidemiological features, genetic evolution, and interactions with host populations. They compared EIV with human influenza viruses to identify parallels and differences in transmission and pathogenesis.
Main results
The study highlights that equine influenza virus has provided critical insights into influenza virus ecology, evolution, and host interactions, particularly due to its unique epidemiological characteristics resembling those of human seasonal influenza viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that EIV has been a valuable model for studying influenza, offering a unique perspective on viral transmission and evolution. This is a novel contribution to understanding how animal models can inform human influenza research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that studying equine influenza virus can enhance our understanding of human seasonal influenza, aiding in the development of more effective surveillance, diagnosis, and control strategies for human influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on historical and observational data, which may introduce limitations in the strength of evidence. However, the authors provide a well-supported narrative grounded in multiple documented studies and comparisons with human influenza.

猪肺部的位点特异性 microRNA 反应取决于 H1N1 甲型流感病毒的 宿主 适应

Site-specific microRNA responses in lungs of pigs depend on the host-adaptation of H1N1 influenza A virus
作者： Louise Brogaard, Helena Aagaard Glud, Charlotte Kristensen, Simon Welner, Lars E. Larsen, Kerstin Skovgaard
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的跨物种 传播 是出现新的大流行的主要风险,然而,跨物种 IAV 感染引起的 宿主 分子反应仍未完全确定。MicroRNA (miRNA) 是先天免疫的重要转录后调节因子,但其在相同亚型但不同 宿主 来源的 IAV 菌株感染过程中的作用尚未得到研究。在这里,我们研究了接种源自 2009H1N1 大流行病毒株的猪适应型 (swH1N1) 或人适应型 (huH1N1) IAV 的猪的上肺和下肺位点的肺部 miRNA 反应。对 115 个 miRNA 和选定的 抗病毒药物 基因进行高通量微流控 RT-qPCR 分析,揭示了菌株依赖性差异,其中 33 个 miRNA 在人类适应的 IAV 感染后在猪肺(上肺和下肺)中差异表达,而在猪适应的 IAV 感染后只有 8 个 miRNA 发生改变。huH1N1 感染后,包括 miR-19a/b、miR-21-5p 和 miR-93-3p 在内的几种 miRNA 的表达与预测的靶模式识别受体 (PRR) 和干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 转录物呈负相关,表明 miRNA 介导的 抗病毒药物 信号传导的微调。值得注意的是,尽管 huH1N1 病毒肺载量与 swH1N1 病毒载量相当,但 huH1N1 感染后下肺中 miRNA 的广泛下调与 ISG 的强劲诱导同时发生。相比之下,猪适应的 IAV 触发了更强的 RIG-I 样受体和 cGAS 转录,而没有实质性的 miRNA 变化。总之,这些发现表明,肺部 miRNA 对 IAV 的反应是毒株和肺部位点特异性的,并且可能有助于根据病毒 宿主 适应的程度来塑造感染的结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的跨物种 传播 是出现新的大流行的主要风险,然而,跨物种 IAV 感染引起的 宿主 分子反应仍未完全确定。
设计与对象
MicroRNA (miRNA) 是先天免疫的重要转录后调节因子,但其在相同亚型但不同 宿主 来源的 IAV 菌株感染过程中的作用尚未得到研究。
核心方法
在这里,我们研究了接种源自 2009H1N1 大流行病毒株的猪适应型 (swH1N1) 或人适应型 (huH1N1) IAV 的猪的上肺和下肺位点的肺部 miRNA 反应。
主要结果
对 115 个 miRNA 和选定的 抗病毒药物 基因进行高通量微流控 RT-qPCR 分析,揭示了菌株依赖性差异,其中 33 个 miRNA 在人类适应的 IAV 感染后在猪肺(上肺和下肺)中差异表达,而在猪适应的 IAV 感染后只有 8 个 miRNA 发生改变。
解释与创新
huH1N1 感染后,包括 miR-19a/b、miR-21-5p 和 miR-93-3p 在内的几种 miRNA 的表达与预测的靶模式识别受体 (PRR) 和干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 转录物呈负相关,表明 miRNA 介导的 抗病毒药物 信号传导的微调。
科研与公卫意义
值得注意的是,尽管 huH1N1 病毒肺载量与 swH1N1 病毒载量相当,但 huH1N1 感染后下肺中 miRNA 的广泛下调与 ISG 的强劲诱导同时发生。
局限与证据强度
相比之下,猪适应的 IAV 触发了更强的 RIG-I 样受体和 cGAS 转录,而没有实质性的 miRNA 变化。

Site-specific microRNA responses in lungs of pigs depend on the host-adaptation of H1N1 influenza A virus

Authors: Louise Brogaard, Helena Aagaard Glud, Charlotte Kristensen, Simon Welner, Lars E. Larsen, Kerstin Skovgaard
Original abstract (English)
Cross-species transmission of influenza A virus (IAV) represents a major risk for emergence of new pandemics, however, the host molecular responses invoked by cross-species IAV infection remain incompletely defined. MicroRNAs (miRNAs) are important post-transcriptional regulators of innate immunity, but their role during infection with IAV strains of the same subtype but of different host origin has not been investigated. Here, we investigated pulmonary miRNA responses in upper and lower lung sites from pigs inoculated with either a swine-adapted (swH1N1) or a human-adapted (huH1N1) IAV originating from the 2009H1N1 pandemic strain. High-throughput microfluidic RT-qPCR profiling of 115 miRNAs and selected antiviral genes revealed strain-dependent differences, with 33 miRNAs being differentially expressed in pig lungs (upper and lower lung) after human-adapted IAV infection, while only eight miRNAs were altered after swine-adapted IAV infection. Expression of several miRNAs correlated inversely with predicted target pattern recognition receptor (PRR) and interferon-stimulated gene (ISG) transcripts after huH1N1 infection, including miR-19a/b, miR-21-5p, and miR-93-3p, suggesting miRNA-mediated fine-tuning of antiviral signaling. Notably, extensive downregulation of miRNAs in the lower lungs after huH1N1 infection coincided with robust ISG induction, despite huH1N1 viral lung load being comparable to swH1N1 viral load. The swine-adapted IAV, in contrast, triggered stronger RIG-I-like receptor and cGAS transcription without substantial miRNA changes. Together, these findings demonstrate that pulmonary miRNA responses to IAV are both strain- and lung site-specific and may contribute to shaping the outcome of infection depending on the degree of viral host adaptation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cross-species transmission of influenza A virus (IAV) represents a major risk for emergence of new pandemics, however, the host molecular responses invoked by cross-species IAV infection remain incompletely defined.

甲型流感病毒感染期间神经氨酸酶和分泌途径应激汇聚重塑 宿主 糖基化

Neuraminidase and secretory pathway stress converge to remodel host glycosylation during influenza A virus infection
作者： Kyle L. Macauslane, Cassandra L. Pegg, Joy Seitanidou, Georgina McCallum, Lauren E. Steele, Melanie Wu, Julian D. J. Sng, Ellesandra C. Noye, Merrina Anugraham, Daniel Kolarich
摘要中文翻译摘要 糖基化对于甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 感染中病毒与 宿主 细胞相互作用至关重要,但我们对 IAV 感染如何塑造 宿主 糖蛋白组缺乏全面的了解。在这里,我们对人 A549 细胞体外时程感染的动态亚细胞反应进行了液相色谱-串联质谱 (LC-MS/MS) 蛋白质组、糖组和糖蛋白质组表征。IAV 感染仅导致亚细胞蛋白质组发生适度变化,但导致 宿主 分泌的细胞器糖组和糖蛋白质组发生强烈且显着的变化。感染导致 N- 和 O-糖(蛋白质)组唾液酸化减少;增加寡甘露糖、寡甘露糖和磷酸化聚糖;和较短的杂合/复合聚糖。当聚糖被岩藻糖基化时,去唾液酸作用更强;对于连接α2,3或α3臂上的唾液酸;较大、复杂的聚糖;以及 IAV 神经氨酸酶 (NA) 更容易接触的蛋白质。感染中未折叠蛋白反应的微妙激活与寡甘露糖 N-糖基化的加倍有关。感染中的聚糖较短,表明 IAV 诱导的高尔基体糖蛋白通量破坏是一种减少 宿主 糖蛋白唾液酸化并促进病毒颗粒释放的机制,与 NA 活性无关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探究甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染如何改变宿主糖蛋白组,因糖基化对病毒-宿主相互作用至关重要,但其机制仍不甚明确。
设计与对象
该研究使用人类 A549 细胞的体外时程感染模型,采用液相色谱-串联质谱 (LC-MS/MS) 来表征 IAV 感染期间的蛋白质组、糖组和糖蛋白质组变化。
核心方法
研究人员进行了 LC-MS/MS 蛋白质组、糖组和糖蛋白质组分析,以检查 IAV 感染的动态亚细胞反应,重点关注聚糖结构的变化及其与 宿主 细胞过程的关联。
主要结果
IAV 感染导致 N- 和 O- 糖(蛋白质)组唾液酸化减少,寡甘露糖、寡甘露糖和磷酸化聚糖以及较短的杂合/复合聚糖增加。未折叠蛋白反应的微妙激活与寡甘露糖 N-糖基化的加倍有关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,IAV 诱导的高尔基体糖蛋白流的破坏减少了 宿主 糖蛋白唾液酸化,并促进病毒颗粒释放,与神经氨酸酶活性无关,为病毒复制提供了一种新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 IAV 感染的分子机制的见解,这可以为靶向治疗的开发提供信息,并提高对 宿主 与 季节性流感 中病毒相互作用的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外模型,可能无法完全代表体内条件,并且证据基于蛋白质组学和糖组学分析,没有直接的功能验证,因此可信度中等。

Neuraminidase and secretory pathway stress converge to remodel host glycosylation during influenza A virus infection

Authors: Kyle L. Macauslane, Cassandra L. Pegg, Joy Seitanidou, Georgina McCallum, Lauren E. Steele, Melanie Wu, Julian D. J. Sng, Ellesandra C. Noye, Merrina Anugraham, Daniel Kolarich
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Glycosylation is critical for viral-host cell interactions in influenza A virus (IAV) infection, but we lack a comprehensive understanding of how IAV infection shapes the host glycoproteome. Here, we performed liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (LC-MS/MS) proteomic, glycomic, and glycoproteomic characterisation of the dynamic subcellular responses to an in vitro time course infection of human A549 cells. IAV infection resulted in only modest changes to the subcellular proteome, but robust and significant changes to the host secreted and organelle glycome and glycoproteome. Infection caused reductions in sialylation across the N- and O-glyco(proteo)me; increased oligomannose, paucimannose, and phosphorylated glycans; and shorter hybrid/complex glycans. Desialylation was greater when glycans were fucosylated; for sialic acid linked α2,3 or on the α3 arm; on larger, complex glycans; and on proteins likely to be more accessible to IAV neuraminidase (NA). Subtle activation of the unfolded protein response in infection was associated with a doubling of oligomannose N-glycosylation. Glycans were shorter in infection, implicating IAV-induced disruption of Golgi glycoprotein flux as a mechanism that reduces host glycoprotein sialylation and promotes virion release, independent of NA activity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how influenza A virus (IAV) infection alters the host glycoproteome, as glycosylation is essential for viral-host interactions but the mechanisms remain poorly understood.
Design and population
The study used an in vitro time course infection model of human A549 cells, employing liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (LC-MS/MS) to characterise proteomic, glycomic, and glycoproteomic changes during IAV infection.
Core methods
The researchers conducted LC-MS/MS proteomic, glycomic, and glycoproteomic analyses to examine the dynamic subcellular responses to IAV infection, focusing on changes in glycan structures and their association with host cell processes.
Main results
IAV infection led to reductions in sialylation across N- and O-glyco(proteo)me, increases in oligomannose, paucimannose, and phosphorylated glycans, and shorter hybrid/complex glycans. Subtle activation of the unfolded protein response was linked to a doubling of oligomannose N-glycosylation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that IAV-induced disruption of Golgi glycoprotein flux reduces host glycoprotein sialylation and facilitates virion release, independent of neuraminidase activity, offering a novel mechanism for viral replication.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of IAV infection, which could inform the development of targeted therapies and improve understanding of host-virus interactions in seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions, and the evidence is based on proteomic and glycomic analyses without direct functional validation, resulting in moderate confidence.

儿童首次免疫印记对禽流感A(H5)病毒的全身性和鼻腔抗体水平存在差异

Pre-existing systemic and nasal antibodies against avian H5 influenza A viruses vary according to childhood imprinting
作者： Peta Edler, Kevin John Selva, Ellie Reilly, Malet Aban, Ian Barr, Jennifer Juno, Adam Wheatley, Amy Chung, Stephen J. Kent, David Price
摘要中文翻译甲型禽流感病毒 (IAV) 构成持续的大流行威胁,最近的 H5 亚型 2.3.4.4b 分支具有高致病性并在动物物种和地理位置中传播。了解人类对禽类 H5 IAV 已有的免疫力可以了解人群的易感性,这是大流行防范的一个重要方面。为此,我们分析了 1928 年至 1999 年间出生、早期接触过不同 IAV 亚型的个体的 IAV HA 特异性抗体。1957 年之前出生的个体对第 1 组 HA 抗原(包括 2.3.4.4b H5 和第 1 组 HA 干抗原)的预先存在的血清抗体最高。这些出生年份特异性模式并未反映在针对 2.3.4.4b H5 IAV 或 H5 特异性记忆 B 细胞群中可检测到的有限的预先存在的血清中和抗体中。然而,它们在预先存在的 H5 鼻腔 IgG 和 IgA 滴度中很明显,这些滴度在 1957 年之前出生的个体中更高。我们的研究结果表明,早期接触带来的免疫偏差延伸到鼻粘膜(IAV 复制部位)中检测到的抗体。重要性了解对大流行潜力的甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的预先存在的免疫力是大流行防范的一个重要方面。这包括了解人群中预先存在的免疫力的异质性。在这里,我们证明了预先存在的 H5 IAV 抗体根据出生年份和童年印记而变化。我们证明全身抗体和鼻抗体都是如此,强调了了解流感病毒复制部位预先存在的粘膜免疫的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型禽流感病毒 (IAV) 构成大流行威胁,了解人类对禽 H5 IAV 已有的免疫力对于预防大流行至关重要。该研究旨在确定预先存在的 H5 IAV 抗体是否会根据童年印记和早年接触 IAV 亚型而变化。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 1928 年至 1999 年间出生的个体的 IAV HA 特异性抗体,并按出生年份分层,以反映不同的早期 IAV 亚型暴露情况。选择参与者来检查对 H5 IAV 的全身和鼻腔抗体反应。
核心方法
分析血清和鼻腔样本中的 HA 特异性抗体,包括血清中和抗体、记忆 B 细胞群以及针对 H5 IAV 的鼻腔 IgG 和 IgA 滴度。测量抗体水平以评估预先存在的免疫力。
主要结果
1957 年之前出生的个体表现出针对第 1 组 HA 抗原(包括 H5 和第 1 组 HA 干抗原)的最高现有血清抗体。然而,这些出生年份特异性模式并未反映在针对 H5 IAV 或 H5 特异性记忆 B 细胞群的血清中和抗体中。1957 年之前出生的个体中预先存在的 H5 鼻腔 IgG 和 IgA 滴度更高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,生命早期接触造成的免疫偏差延伸到鼻粘膜(IAV 复制的主要部位)中检测到的抗体。这凸显了了解粘膜免疫在大流行防范中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,童年印记影响预先存在的对 H5 IAV 的粘膜免疫,这可能为针对大流行威胁的 监测 策略和 疫苗 设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其关注的是预先存在的抗体,并且没有涉及功能性免疫或针对感染的保护。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于横截面抽样并且没有建立因果关系。

Pre-existing systemic and nasal antibodies against avian H5 influenza A viruses vary according to childhood imprinting

Authors: Peta Edler, Kevin John Selva, Ellie Reilly, Malet Aban, Ian Barr, Jennifer Juno, Adam Wheatley, Amy Chung, Stephen J. Kent, David Price
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A viruses (IAVs) pose a constant pandemic threat, with the recent 2.3.4.4b clade of the H5 subtype causing high pathogenicity and spreading across animal species and geographic locations. Understanding human pre-existing immunity to avian H5 IAV can inform on population susceptibility, a critical aspect of pandemic preparedness. To that end, we analyzed the IAV HA-specific antibodies across individuals born between 1928 and 1999 with different early life exposures to IAV subtypes. Individuals born prior to 1957 had the highest pre-existing serum antibodies to group 1 HA antigens, including the 2.3.4.4b H5 and a group 1 HA stem antigen. These birth year-specific patterns were not reflected in the limited pre-existing serum neutralizing antibodies detectable against a 2.3.4.4b H5 IAV or in H5-specific memory B cell populations. They were, however, evident in pre-existing nasal IgG and IgA titers to H5, which were greater in individuals born prior to 1957. Our findings demonstrate that the immunological biases afforded by early life exposure extend to antibodies detected in the nasal mucosa, the site of IAV replication.IMPORTANCEUnderstating pre-existing immunity to influenza A viruses (IAVs) of pandemic potential is an important aspect of pandemic preparedness. This includes an understanding of the heterogeneity of pre-existing immunity across the population. Here, we demonstrate that pre-existing antibodies to H5 IAV vary according to the year of birth and childhood imprinting. We demonstrate that this is the case for both systemic and nasal antibodies, highlighting the importance of understanding pre-existing mucosal immunity at the sites of influenza virus replication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A viruses (IAVs) pose a pandemic threat, and understanding human pre-existing immunity to avian H5 IAV is critical for pandemic preparedness. The study aimed to determine whether pre-existing antibodies to H5 IAV vary according to childhood imprinting and early life exposure to IAV subtypes.
Design and population
The study analyzed IAV HA-specific antibodies in individuals born between 1928 and 1999, stratified by birth year to reflect different early life exposures to IAV subtypes. Participants were selected to examine systemic and nasal antibody responses to H5 IAV.
Core methods
Serum and nasal samples were analyzed for HA-specific antibodies, including serum neutralizing antibodies, memory B cell populations, and nasal IgG and IgA titers against H5 IAV. Antibody levels were measured to assess pre-existing immunity.
Main results
Individuals born prior to 1957 exhibited the highest pre-existing serum antibodies to group 1 HA antigens, including H5 and a group 1 HA stem antigen. However, these birth year-specific patterns were not reflected in serum neutralizing antibodies against H5 IAV or H5-specific memory B cell populations. Pre-existing nasal IgG and IgA titers to H5 were greater in individuals born prior to 1957.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that immunological biases from early life exposure extend to antibodies detected in the nasal mucosa, the primary site of IAV replication. This highlights the importance of understanding mucosal immunity in pandemic preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that childhood imprinting influences pre-existing mucosal immunity to H5 IAV, which may inform surveillance strategies and vaccine design targeting pandemic threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on pre-existing antibodies and does not address functional immunity or protection against infection. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on cross-sectional sampling and does not establish causality.

打破流感神话和医护人员对不良反应的恐惧:关于 疫苗接种 意图的两项实验研究。

Myth-busting influenza and fear of adverse reactions in healthcare workers: two experimental studies on vaccination intentions.
作者： Lewis Bott, Jia-Wen Liu, Aimee Challenger, Eryl Powell, Petroc Sumner
摘要中文翻译背景 季节性流感 造成严重的全球危害,为医护人员 (HCW) 接种疫苗对于保护工作人员和患者至关重要。然而,医护人员流感 疫苗接种 率仍低于目标。误解,尤其是对不良反应的担忧,经常被认为是障碍,许多运动都使用打破神话的材料来反驳这些信念。这项研究测试了纠正流感神话和解决对不良反应的担忧是否会导致 疫苗 意图的增加。方法进行了两项在线实验研究。医护人员参与者是主要关注点,但也包括一般人群样本以进行比较。在实验 1 中(医护人员 n = 514;普通人群 n = 401),参与者观看了揭穿常见流感神话的图像,并随机分配材料,包括或排除解决不良反应的附加图像。实验 2(HCW n = 535;普通人群 n = 1200)专门关注不良反应消息传递,参与者被随机分配到四种图像格式之一。疫苗接种 意图是主要结果。结果在实验 1 中,打破神话显着增加了两个样本中的 疫苗接种 意图(均 p 0.12)。添加不良反应图像不会产生额外的增加。在实验 2 中,HCW 没有出现总体效应,但显着的交互作用 (p = .011,ηp 2 = 0.04) 表明效果因图像而异:一个增加了意图 (p = .029,d z = 0.25),两个显示无效效应,一个产生了边际逆火效应 (p = .052 d z = -0.21)。在一般人群样本中,意图总体增加(p < .001,d z = 0.15),且没有交互作用。结论 在观看打破神话的材料后,疫苗接种 意图确实比以前更高,但控制意图却没有。因此,打破谣言是增加流感 疫苗接种 意向的有效方法。相比之下,明确解决轻微不良反应并不能可靠地提高意图,并且对于一组材料来说,会略微降低意图。因此,针对医护人员的活动应在实施前测试不良反应信息。

问题与背景
季节性流感 造成严重的全球危害,为医护人员 (HCW) 接种疫苗对于保护工作人员和患者至关重要。然而,医护人员流感 疫苗接种 率仍低于目标。
设计与对象
进行了两项在线实验研究。医护人员参与者是主要关注点,但也包括一般人群样本以进行比较。在实验 1 中(医护人员 n = 514;普通人群 n = 401),参与者观看了揭穿常见流感神话的图像,并随机分配材料,包括或排除解决不良反应的附加图像。
核心方法
该研究涉及两项在线实验研究。参与者被随机观看不同的图像材料,包括揭穿流感神话和解决不良反应的图像材料。疫苗接种 意图是测量的主要结果。
主要结果
在实验 1 中,打破神话显着增加了两个样本中的 疫苗接种 意图(均 p 0.12)。添加不良反应图像不会产生额外的增加。在实验 2 中,HCW 没有出现总体效应,但显着的交互作用 (p = .011,ηp 2 = 0.04) 表明效果因图像而异:一个增加了意图 (p = .029,d z = 0.25),两个显示无效效应,一个产生了边际逆火效应 (p = .052,d z = -0.21)。在一般人群样本中,意图总体增加(p < .001,d z = 0.15),且没有交互作用。

因此,针对医护人员的活动应在实施前测试不良反应信息。

所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Myth-busting influenza and fear of adverse reactions in healthcare workers: two experimental studies on vaccination intentions.

Authors: Lewis Bott, Jia-Wen Liu, Aimee Challenger, Eryl Powell, Petroc Sumner
Original abstract (English)
Background Seasonal influenza causes significant global harms, and vaccinating healthcare workers (HCW) is central to protecting both staff and patients. However, HCW influenza vaccination rates remain below targets. Misconceptions, particularly concerns about adverse reactions, are frequently cited barriers, and many campaigns use myth-busting materials to counter these beliefs. This study tested whether correcting influenza myths and addressing concerns about adverse reactions leads to an increase in vaccine intentions. Methods Two online experimental studies were conducted. HCW participants were of primary interest but a general population sample was included for comparison. In Experiment 1 (HCW n = 514; general population n = 401), participants viewed images debunking common influenza myths, with randomisation to materials that either included or excluded an additional image addressing adverse reactions. Experiment 2 (HCW n = 535; general population n = 1200) focused exclusively on adverse reaction messaging, with participants randomised to one of four image formats. Vaccination intention was the primary outcome. Results In Experiment 1, myth-busting significantly increased vaccination intentions (all p 0.12) in both samples. Adding an adverse reactions image produced no additional increase. In Experiment 2, no overall effects emerged for HCW, but a significant interaction ( p = .011, ηp 2 = 0.04) indicated that effects varied by image: one increased intentions ( p = .029, d z = 0.25), two showed null effects, and one produced a marginal backfire effect ( p = .052 d z = −0.21). In the general population sample, intentions increased overall ( p < .001, d z = 0.15) with no interaction. Conclusions Vaccination intentions were reliably higher after viewing myth-busting materials than before, but control intentions were not. Myth-busting is therefore an effective approach to increasing influenza vaccination intentions. In contrast, explicitly addressing mild adverse reactions did not reliably raise intentions and, for one set of materials, marginally reduced them. Campaigns targeting HCW should therefore test adverse reaction messaging before implementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza causes significant global harms, and vaccinating healthcare workers (HCW) is central to protecting both staff and patients. However, HCW influenza vaccination rates remain below targets.
Design and population
Two online experimental studies were conducted. HCW participants were of primary interest but a general population sample was included for comparison. In Experiment 1 (HCW n = 514; general population n = 401), participants viewed images debunking common influenza myths, with randomisation to materials that either included or excluded an additional image addressing adverse reactions.
Core methods
The study involved two online experimental studies. Participants were randomised to view different image materials, including those debunking influenza myths and addressing adverse reactions. Vaccination intention was the primary outcome measured.
Main results
In Experiment 1, myth-busting significantly increased vaccination intentions (all p 0.12) in both samples. Adding an adverse reactions image produced no additional increase. In Experiment 2, no overall effects emerged for HCW, but a significant interaction (p = .011, ηp 2 = 0.04) indicated that effects varied by image: one increased intentions (p = .029, d z = 0.25), two showed null effects, and one produced a marginal backfire effect (p = .052, d z = −0.21). In the general population sample, intentions increased overall (p < .001, d z = 0.15) with no interaction.
Interpretation and novelty
Vaccination intentions were reliably higher after viewing myth-busting materials than before, but control intentions were not. Myth-busting is therefore an effective approach to increasing influenza vaccination intentions.
Scientific and public-health significance
Campaigns targeting HCW should therefore test adverse reaction messaging before implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

甲型 H1N1 流感感染通过人脑微血管内皮细胞中的 TNF-α-组织因子-凝血酶信号触发内皮免疫血栓形成

Influenza A H1N1 Infection Triggers Endothelial Immunothrombosis via TNF-α–Tissue Factor–Thrombin Signaling in Human Brain Microvascular Endothelial Cells
作者： Luis A. Pérez-Moreno, Silvia Guzmán-Beltrán, Víctor A. Hernández-Hernández, Blanca H. Ruiz-Ordaz, Belen Romo-Espinosa, Carlos Cabello-Gutiérrez
摘要中文翻译严重甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 感染与内皮功能障碍、炎症和凝血异常有关。然而,内皮炎症激活与血栓形成之间的联系机制仍不完全清楚。我们研究了甲型 H1N1 流感是否会在人脑微血管内皮细胞中诱导免疫血栓表型。我们用甲型流感/H1N1 pdm09 感染 HBEC-5i 细胞。我们通过 RT-qPCR 检测基质 (M) 转录物和 M1 蛋白的免疫印迹来评估病毒感染。我们通过测量 TNF-α 表达和分泌、组织因子 (TF) 表达、TF 依赖性因子 Xa 生成以及对重组 TNF-α 和凝血酶的反应来评估内皮反应。H1N1 感染增加了 TNF-α 的表达和分泌,并增强了 TF 的表达和功能活性,表明获得了促凝血内皮表型。重组 TNF-α 再现了感染相关的 TF 诱导,而凝血酶增强了 TNF-α 的产生和 TF 活性,支持炎症和凝血途径之间的相互放大。这些发现支持 TNF-α-TF-凝血酶前馈信号回路在 H1N1 感染期间将内皮炎症和凝血联系起来。甲型 H1N1 流感在人脑微血管内皮细胞中诱导免疫血栓表型,将该信号轴确定为流感相关血管和血栓并发症的潜在机制靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染与内皮功能障碍、炎症和凝血异常有关,但内皮炎症激活与血栓形成之间的联系机制仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象
该研究通过用甲型 H1N1 流感 pdm09 感染人脑微血管内皮细胞 (HBEC-5i) 来调查甲型 H1N1 流感是否会在这些细胞中诱导免疫血栓表型。
核心方法
通过 RT-qPCR 检测基质 (M) 转录物和 M1 蛋白免疫印迹证实病毒感染。通过测量 TNF-α 表达和分泌、组织因子 (TF) 表达、TF 依赖性因子 Xa 生成以及对重组 TNF-α 和凝血酶的反应来评估内皮反应。
主要结果
H1N1 感染增加了 TNF-α 的表达和分泌,并增强了 TF 的表达和功能活性,表明获得了促凝血内皮表型。
解释与创新
研究结果支持 TNF-α-TF-凝血酶前馈信号通路在 H1N1 感染期间将内皮炎症和凝血联系起来,并将该信号轴确定为流感相关血管和血栓并发症的潜在机制靶标。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,针对 TNF-α-TF-凝血酶轴可以减轻流感相关的血管和血栓并发症,为未来的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于使用单个细胞系和病毒株的体外实验,这限制了对临床环境和其他流感亚型的普遍性。

Influenza A H1N1 Infection Triggers Endothelial Immunothrombosis via TNF-α–Tissue Factor–Thrombin Signaling in Human Brain Microvascular Endothelial Cells

Authors: Luis A. Pérez-Moreno, Silvia Guzmán-Beltrán, Víctor A. Hernández-Hernández, Blanca H. Ruiz-Ordaz, Belen Romo-Espinosa, Carlos Cabello-Gutiérrez
Original abstract (English)
Severe Influenza A virus (IAV) infection is associated with endothelial dysfunction, inflammation, and coagulation abnormalities. However, the mechanisms linking endothelial inflammatory activation to thrombosis remain incompletely understood. We investigated whether Influenza A/H1N1 induces an immunothrombotic phenotype in human brain microvascular endothelial cells. We infected HBEC-5i cells with Influenza A/H1N1 pdm09. We assessed viral infection by RT-qPCR detection of matrix (M) transcripts and by immunoblotting for the M1 protein. We evaluated endothelial responses by measuring TNF-α expression and secretion, Tissue Factor (TF) expression, TF-dependent factor Xa generation, and responses to recombinant TNF-α and thrombin. H1N1 infection increased TNF-α expression and secretion and enhanced TF expression and functional activity, indicating acquisition of a procoagulant endothelial phenotype. Recombinant TNF-α reproduced infection-associated TF induction, whereas thrombin enhanced TNF-α production and TF activity, supporting reciprocal amplification between inflammatory and coagulation pathways. These findings support a TNF-α–TF–thrombin feed-forward signaling circuit linking endothelial inflammation and coagulation during H1N1 infection. Influenza A/H1N1 induces an immunothrombotic phenotype in human brain microvascular endothelial cells, identifying this signaling axis as a potential mechanistic target for influenza-associated vascular and thrombotic complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe Influenza A virus (IAV) infection is linked to endothelial dysfunction, inflammation, and coagulation abnormalities, but the mechanisms connecting endothelial inflammatory activation to thrombosis remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
The study investigated whether Influenza A/H1N1 induces an immunothrombotic phenotype in human brain microvascular endothelial cells (HBEC-5i) by infecting these cells with Influenza A/H1N1 pdm09.
Core methods
Viral infection was confirmed by RT-qPCR detection of matrix (M) transcripts and immunoblotting for the M1 protein. Endothelial responses were evaluated by measuring TNF-α expression and secretion, Tissue Factor (TF) expression, TF-dependent factor Xa generation, and responses to recombinant TNF-α and thrombin.
Main results
H1N1 infection increased TNF-α expression and secretion and enhanced TF expression and functional activity, indicating acquisition of a procoagulant endothelial phenotype.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support a TNF-α–TF–thrombin feed-forward signaling circuit linking endothelial inflammation and coagulation during H1N1 infection, identifying this signaling axis as a potential mechanistic target for influenza-associated vascular and thrombotic complications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that targeting the TNF-α–TF–thrombin axis could mitigate influenza-associated vascular and thrombotic complications, informing future therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro experiments using a single cell line and viral strain, which constrains generalizability to clinical settings and other influenza subtypes.

快速菌株匹配 疫苗接种 在缓解流感大流行中的临床和公共卫生经济影响:类似英国的人口动态 传播 模型

Clinical and public health economic impact of rapid strain-matched vaccination in influenza pandemic mitigation: A UK-like population dynamic transmission model
作者： Sam Lovick, Malur Sudhanva, Alexander T. Kennedy, Ray Longstaff, Emanuele Ciglia, Joaquin Mould-Quevedo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在量化由于快速部署毒株匹配的大流行性流感 疫苗 而导致的发病率、死亡率 和医疗保健利用率的降低,同时比较 疫苗 平台的结果并评估非药物干预措施 (NPI) 的经济影响及其与 疫苗接种 时机的相互作用。
设计与对象
使用类似英国人群的易感-暴露-感染-恢复 (SEIR) 动态 传播 模型来模拟大流行性流感情景,包括在整个 疫苗 可用时间范围内传播性和严重性的变化。
核心方法
该研究采用 SEIR 模型来模拟大流行性流感动态,结合不同的 疫苗 平台(基于鸡蛋和基于细胞),并评估非药物干预措施 (NPI) 以及 疫苗接种 时机和可用性的经济影响。
主要结果
A 16-week vaccination campaign starting 16 weeks post-pandemic declaration reduced infections by 3.0 million and deaths by 28,000, with greater benefits observed in low-transmissibility scenarios. Delays in vaccine availability increased both mortality and economic costs.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,快速菌株匹配的 疫苗接种 显着减少了感染和死亡,特别是在低传播率的情况下,并且 疫苗 可用性的延迟会对健康结果和经济成本产生不利影响。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明及时部署 疫苗 的重要性,并强调需要采取灵活的策略来应对不同的流行病严重程度和传播性,并对公共卫生规划和资源分配产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于基于模型的方法,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性,并且证据基于模拟场景而不是经验数据,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Clinical and public health economic impact of rapid strain-matched vaccination in influenza pandemic mitigation: A UK-like population dynamic transmission model

Authors: Sam Lovick, Malur Sudhanva, Alexander T. Kennedy, Ray Longstaff, Emanuele Ciglia, Joaquin Mould-Quevedo
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus H5N1 has recently circulated at unprecedented levels in birds and mammals, causing sporadic human infections. Using a susceptible-exposed-infectious-recovered (SEIR) dynamic transmission model of a UK-like population, this study aimed to quantify reductions in morbidity, mortality, and healthcare utilization attributable to rapid deployment of a strain-matched pandemic influenza vaccine; to compare outcomes across egg-based and cell-based vaccine platforms; to evaluate the economic impact of nonpharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) and their interaction with vaccination timing; and to estimate the impact of earlier or delayed vaccine availability across a full spectrum of pandemic transmissibility and severity scenarios. In the absence of NPIs, a 16-week vaccination campaign starting 16 weeks post-pandemic declaration reduced infections by 3.0 million and deaths by 28,000, with greater benefits observed in low-transmissibility scenarios. A cell-based vaccine with equivalent timing further reduced deaths by 5.0%, with additional gains in scenarios involving faster vaccine production. NPIs (average cost, £208 billion) enhanced vaccine effectiveness across all pandemic types, most markedly in high-transmissibility events; cell-based vaccine additionally reduced NPI costs by 0.9% relative to egg-based vaccine. Conversely, delays in vaccine availability increased both mortality and economic costs. These findings highlight the need for robust and agile vaccine development, registration, and distribution infrastructures, as well as flexible response strategies adaptable to varying pandemic severity and transmissibility. Policymakers should consider adopting a comprehensive framework integrating health outcome metrics and the economic impact of NPIs to inform vaccine procurement and deployment decisions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to quantify reductions in morbidity, mortality, and healthcare utilization attributable to rapid deployment of a strain-matched pandemic influenza vaccine, while comparing outcomes across vaccine platforms and evaluating the economic impact of nonpharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) and their interaction with vaccination timing.
Design and population
A susceptible-exposed-infectious-recovered (SEIR) dynamic transmission model of a UK-like population was used to simulate pandemic influenza scenarios, including variations in transmissibility and severity, across a full spectrum of vaccine availability timing.
Core methods
The study employed a SEIR model to simulate pandemic influenza dynamics, incorporating different vaccine platforms (egg-based and cell-based) and evaluating the economic impact of nonpharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) alongside vaccination timing and availability.
Main results
A 16-week vaccination campaign starting 16 weeks post-pandemic declaration reduced infections by 3.0 million and deaths by 28,000, with greater benefits observed in low-transmissibility scenarios. Delays in vaccine availability increased both mortality and economic costs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that rapid strain-matched vaccination significantly reduces infections and deaths, particularly in low-transmissibility scenarios, and that delays in vaccine availability have adverse effects on both health outcomes and economic costs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform the importance of timely vaccine deployment and highlight the need for flexible strategies that account for varying pandemic severity and transmissibility, with implications for public health planning and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a model-based approach, which may not fully capture real-world complexities, and the evidence is based on simulated scenarios rather than empirical data, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

针对流感病毒血凝素-α-2,6-唾液酸相互作用的抑制剂的新型基于细胞的高通量筛选模型

A Novel Cell-Based High-Throughput Screening Model for Inhibitors Targeting Influenza Virus Hemagglutinin–α-2,6-Sialic Acid Interaction
作者： Keyu Guo, Xiaofang Chen, Chenyin Wang, Yaru Liu, Chao Liu, 巫晔翔, 樊秀勇, Yanni Xu, Shuyi Si, Yongxin Zhang
摘要中文翻译耐药性的增加破坏了当前的抗流感病毒疗法。尽管针对血凝素(HA)-唾液酸受体相互作用是一种有前途的策略,但由于缺乏与亚型无关的筛选模型,进展受到阻碍。在此,我们建立了基于荧光的细胞高通量模型,使用异硫氰酸荧光素缀合的黑接骨木凝集素(FITC-SNA)作为稳定的HA替代物和α-2,6-唾液酸转移酶(ST6GAL1)过表达的MDCK细胞来模拟HA-受体界面。该平台旨在作为一种有效的初级筛选工具,快速筛选大型化合物库,寻找受体结合界面的潜在结合剂。通过筛选 10,000 种化合物,确定 Obatoclax Mesylate 和 Ethylparaben 为主要热门产品。两者均表现出广谱 HA 抑制活性,验证了模型识别干扰病毒附着的化合物的能力。进一步的细胞 抗病毒药物 测定揭示了两种化合物的细胞毒性,导致选择性指数 (SI) 较低,表明虽然这些分子有效地靶向相互作用位点,但它们需要大量的结构优化才能用于治疗用途。分子对接证实了它们与 A 型 H1N1、H3N2 和 B/Victoria HA 蛋白的结合,而 ADMET 预测强调了减轻毒性的特定结构优化需求。总之,这种独立于亚型的、高度特异性的高通量筛选(HTS)模型为早期流感药物发现和先导化合物开发提供了高效可靠的平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了由于耐药性上升而对新的抗流感疗法的迫切需求,突显了针对血凝素(HA)-α-2,6-唾液酸相互作用的抑制剂的亚型独立筛选模型中的差距。
设计与对象
作者开发了一种基于荧光的高通量筛选模型,使用 FITC-SNA 作为 HA 和 ST6GAL1 过表达 MDCK 细胞的替代物来模拟受体界面,设计用于大型化合物库的初步筛选。
核心方法
该平台筛选了 10,000 种化合物,以识别受体结合界面的结合物,并通过细胞 抗病毒药物 测定、针对 A 型 H1N1、H3N2 和 B/Victoria HA 蛋白的分子对接以及 ADMET 预测来验证命中,以评估毒性和优化需求。
主要结果
筛选确定 Obatoclax Mesylate 和 Ethylparaben 是具有广谱 HA 抑制活性的主要候选药物,但两者均表现出细胞毒性,导致选择性指数较低,表明需要进行结构优化。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明该模型能够有效识别干扰病毒附着的化合物,并为早期流感药物发现提供独立于亚型的、高度特异性的 HTS 平台。
科研与公卫意义
该模型为加速流感药物发现中的先导化合物开发提供了实用工具,有可能改善针对 HA 受体界面的候选治疗药物的识别。
局限与证据强度
证据受到已识别命中的细胞毒性和低选择性指数的限制,这限制了立即的治疗适用性,并且该研究依赖于体外和计算预测,而没有体内验证。

A Novel Cell-Based High-Throughput Screening Model for Inhibitors Targeting Influenza Virus Hemagglutinin–α-2,6-Sialic Acid Interaction

Authors: Keyu Guo, Xiaofang Chen, Chenyin Wang, Yaru Liu, Chao Liu, 巫晔翔, 樊秀勇, Yanni Xu, Shuyi Si, Yongxin Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Rising drug resistance undermines current anti-influenza virus therapies. Although targeting the hemagglutinin (HA)–sialic acid receptor interaction is a promising strategy, progress is impeded by the lack of subtype-independent screening models. Herein, we established a fluorescence-based cell high-throughput model using fluorescein isothiocyanate-conjugated Sambucus Nigra Lectin (FITC-SNA) as a stable HA surrogate and α-2,6-sialyltransferase (ST6GAL1)-overexpressing MDCK cells to mimic the HA–receptor interface. This platform was designed to serve as an efficient primary screening tool to rapidly filter large compound libraries for potential binders to the receptor-binding interface. Screening 10,000 compounds identified Obatoclax Mesylate and Ethylparaben as primary hits. Both exhibited broad-spectrum HA inhibition activity, validating the model’s capability to identify compounds interfering with viral attachment. Further cellular antiviral assays revealed cytotoxicity for both compounds, resulting in low selectivity indexes (SIs), indicating that while these molecules effectively target the interaction site, they require substantial structural optimization for therapeutic use. Molecular docking confirmed their binding to type A H1N1, H3N2, and B/Victoria HA proteins, while ADMET predictions highlighted specific structural optimization needs to mitigate toxicity. In conclusion, this subtype-independent, highly specific high-throughput screening (HTS) model provides an efficient and reliable platform for early-stage influenza drug discovery and lead compound development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent need for new anti-influenza therapies due to rising drug resistance, highlighting a gap in subtype-independent screening models for inhibitors targeting the hemagglutinin (HA)–α-2,6-sialic acid interaction.
Design and population
The authors developed a fluorescence-based high-throughput screening model using FITC-SNA as a surrogate for HA and ST6GAL1-overexpressing MDCK cells to mimic the receptor interface, designed for primary screening of large compound libraries.
Core methods
The platform screened 10,000 compounds to identify binders to the receptor-binding interface, with hits validated through cellular antiviral assays, molecular docking against type A H1N1, H3N2, and B/Victoria HA proteins, and ADMET predictions to assess toxicity and optimization needs.
Main results
Screening identified Obatoclax Mesylate and Ethylparaben as primary hits with broad-spectrum HA inhibition activity, but both exhibited cytotoxicity resulting in low selectivity indexes, indicating the need for structural optimization.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the model’s capability to efficiently identify compounds interfering with viral attachment, with novelty in providing a subtype-independent, highly specific HTS platform for early-stage influenza drug discovery.
Scientific and public-health significance
This model offers a practical tool for accelerating lead compound development in influenza drug discovery, potentially improving the identification of therapeutic candidates targeting the HA–receptor interface.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the cytotoxicity and low selectivity indexes of identified hits, which constrain immediate therapeutic applicability, and the study relies on in vitro and computational predictions without in vivo validation.

急性病毒感染加速小鼠模型中肌萎缩侧索硬化症的神经退行

Acute viral infection accelerates neurodegeneration in a mouse model of ALS
作者： Art Marzok, Jonathan Mapletoft, Imran Ahmed, Braeden Cowbrough, Daniel B. Celeste, Michael R. D’Agostino, Jann C. Ang, Andrew Chen, Vithushan Surendran, Yona Tugg
摘要中文翻译摘要 虽然多种病毒感染与肌萎缩侧索硬化症 (ALS) 有关,但它们促进疾病的机制仍然几乎完全难以捉摸。本研究调查了 SOD1 G93A 小鼠模型中疾病发作前常见的急性病毒感染对 ALS 进展的影响。ALS 临床症状出现前的一次亚致死感染与 ALS 疾病进展显着加速相关,其特征是后肢功能迅速丧失。先前的感染导致腰椎神经胶质增生以及涉及炎症反应、代谢失调和肌肉功能障碍的转录途径上调。使用抗炎小分子治疗性抑制神经胶质增生或施用直接作用的 抗病毒药物 与 ALS 临床症状的显着改善相关,类似于在未感染动物中观察到的情况。这项研究提供了因果和机制证据,表明急性病毒感染引起的 免疫应答 可能是改变 ALS 疾病轨迹的重要病因,并为 ALS 的新治疗和预防策略提供了见解。一句话总结 甲型流感病毒和 SARS-CoV-2 的急性病毒感染加速了 SOD1 G93A 小鼠 ALS 的进展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过检查急性病毒感染在疾病机制中的潜在作用,探讨急性病毒感染如何影响肌萎缩侧索硬化症(ALS)的进展。
设计与对象
本研究使用 SOD1 G93A 小鼠模型来评估常见急性病毒感染对 ALS 进展的影响,重点关注疾病发作前的时期。
核心方法
研究人员在小鼠出现 ALS 临床症状之前诱导了一次亚致死病毒感染,并监测了疾病的进展。他们还评估了针对神经胶质增生和病毒感染的治疗干预措施的效果。
主要结果
ALS 发病前的一次亚致死感染与疾病进展加速有关,其特点是后肢功能迅速丧失。治疗性抑制神经胶质增生或给予 抗病毒药物 可改善临床体征。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,由急性病毒感染引发的 免疫应答 可能会显着改变 ALS 的病程,为潜在的治疗和预防策略提供新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了在 ALS 研究中考虑病毒感染的重要性,并可能为未来关于炎症和 抗病毒药物 疗法在疾病管理中的作用的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,证据基于单一感染类型,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,这些结果得到了直接观察和干预的支持,提供了适度的信心。

Acute viral infection accelerates neurodegeneration in a mouse model of ALS

Authors: Art Marzok, Jonathan Mapletoft, Imran Ahmed, Braeden Cowbrough, Daniel B. Celeste, Michael R. D’Agostino, Jann C. Ang, Andrew Chen, Vithushan Surendran, Yona Tugg
Original abstract (English)
Summary While several viral infections have been associated with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS), the mechanism(s) through which they promote disease has remained almost entirely elusive. This study investigated the impact of common, acute viral infections prior to disease onset on ALS progression in the SOD1 G93A mouse model. A single sublethal infection prior to onset of ALS clinical signs was associated with markedly accelerated ALS disease progression characterized by rapid loss of hindlimb function. Prior infection resulted in gliosis in the lumbar spine and upregulation of transcriptional pathways involved in inflammatory responses, metabolic dysregulation, and muscular dysfunction. Therapeutic suppression of gliosis with an anti-inflammatory small molecule, or administration of a direct-acting antiviral, was associated with significantly improved ALS clinical signs, akin to what was observed in uninfected animals. This study provides causal and mechanistic evidence that the immune response elicited by acute viral infections may be an important etiological factor that alters ALS disease trajectory, and provides insight into novel therapeutic and preventative strategies for ALS. One Sentence Summary Acute viral infection with influenza A virus and SARS-CoV-2 accelerates the progression of ALS in SOD1 G93A mice.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how acute viral infections might influence the progression of amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) by examining their potential role in disease mechanisms.
Design and population
This study used a SOD1 G93A mouse model to assess the impact of common acute viral infections on ALS progression, focusing on the period before disease onset.
Core methods
The researchers induced a single sublethal viral infection in mice prior to the onset of ALS clinical signs and monitored the progression of the disease. They also evaluated the effects of therapeutic interventions targeting gliosis and viral infection.
Main results
A single sublethal infection prior to ALS onset was associated with accelerated disease progression, marked by rapid hindlimb function loss. Therapeutic suppression of gliosis or administration of antivirals improved clinical signs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the immune response triggered by acute viral infections may significantly alter the course of ALS, offering new insights into potential therapeutic and preventative strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the importance of considering viral infections in ALS research and may inform future studies on the role of inflammation and antiviral therapies in disease management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mouse model, and the evidence is based on a single infection type, which may affect generalizability. However, the results are supported by direct observations and interventions, providing moderate confidence.

城市污水与环境监测:来自乌干达坎帕拉有/无污水管网卫生设施的证据

Wastewater and Environmental Surveillance in Cities with Sewered and Non-sewered Sanitation: Evidence from Kampala, Uganda
作者： Seju Kang, Annet Kagene, Gava Job Ssazi Pius, Jude Zziwa Byansi, Gloria Mirembe, Flavia Zabali Musisi, Charles B. Niwagaba, Karin Gallandat, Timothy R. Julian, Linda Strande
摘要中文翻译研究背景:污水与环境监测(WES)可用于社区层面的传染病监测。目前大部分进展集中于基于污水管网的监测,但全球近一半人口依赖无污水管网的卫生设施。在无污水管网的环境中,城市排水渠道已被用于脊髓灰质炎病毒的环境监测,但其在多病原体WES中的潜力、空间定义的汇水区域以及与基于污水管网的监测的可比性仍有待探索。方法:在坎帕拉市内选择了10个排水渠道采样点,并划定了微汇水区域(0.95-3.83 km2,覆盖12,885-44,146人)。在2025年3月及9-10月的两次采样活动中,共收集了255份排水渠道样本和54份污水处理厂(WWTP)进水样本。通过数字PCR定量检测多靶标面板,包括肠道病毒(诺如病毒GI和GII、轮状病毒)、呼吸道病毒(SARS-CoV-2、甲型和乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV))、非O1/O139霍乱弧菌(V. cholerae)以及作为粪便指示剂的辣椒轻斑驳病毒(PMMoV)。研究结果:肠道病毒、非O1/O139霍乱弧菌和PMMoV在所有排水渠道及WWTP进水样本中均被持续检出。除非O1/O139霍乱弧菌外,排水渠道中的浓度通常低于WWTP进水样本。在以PMMoV标准化后,大多数排水渠道的浓度与WWTP进水样本相当,但不同靶标和地点的可比性存在差异。浓度及PMMoV标准化后的浓度在采样点间存在空间变异。两次采样活动的趋势因病原体靶标而异,且无法用单一微汇水区域特征解释。甲型流感病毒是最常检出的呼吸道病毒(排水渠道中占8-23%,WWTP进水中占3-10%),而SARS-CoV-2、乙型流感病毒和RSV的检出率较低。结论:大多数微汇水区域的PMMoV标准化浓度与WWTP进水样本相当,空间异质性表明疾病患病率在社区层面存在差异。划定汇水区域的排水渠道监测有潜力在无污水管网环境中提供与基于污水管网的废水监测相当的空间分辨率公共卫生信息。资助:Eawag自主资金
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估无下水道环境中城市排水渠道对多病原体废水和环境 监测 的潜力,解决在了解其与基于下水道系统的可比性方面的差距。
设计与对象
该研究在 2025 年 3 月和 9 月至 10 月的两次活动期间在乌干达坎帕拉进行,涉及 10 个具有明确微集水区的排水沟采样点和 255 个排水沟样品以及 54 个废水 治疗 工厂进水样品。
核心方法
使用数字 PCR 对多目标组进行定量,包括肠道病毒、呼吸道病毒(例如甲型和乙型流感)、SARS-CoV-2 和 RSV,以及作为粪便指标的非 O1/O139 霍乱弧菌和 PMMoV。
主要结果
在所有排水渠道和污水处理厂进水中均检测到肠道病毒、非 O1/O139 霍乱弧菌和 PMMoV。甲型流感病毒是最常检测到的呼吸道病毒,在排水沟中占 8-23%,在污水处理厂进水中占 3-10%,而 SARS-CoV-2、乙型流感和 RSV 则很少检测到。
解释与创新
大多数微流域的 PMMoV 标准化浓度与污水处理厂进水相当,这表明基于排水的 监测 可以在无下水道环境中提供空间解析的公共卫生信息。
科研与公卫意义
流域划定的排水 监测 有潜力在无下水道环境中提供空间解析的公共卫生信息,与基于下水道的废水监测相当,这可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果因采样地点和活动的不同而有所不同,并且趋势不能用任何单一的微流域特征来解释,这表明空间普遍性的潜在局限性以及需要进一步验证。

Wastewater and Environmental Surveillance in Cities with Sewered and Non-sewered Sanitation: Evidence from Kampala, Uganda

Authors: Seju Kang, Annet Kagene, Gava Job Ssazi Pius, Jude Zziwa Byansi, Gloria Mirembe, Flavia Zabali Musisi, Charles B. Niwagaba, Karin Gallandat, Timothy R. Julian, Linda Strande
Original abstract (English)
Summary Background: Wastewater and environmental surveillance (WES) enables community-level monitoring of infectious diseases. Most progress has focused on sewer-based surveillance, yet nearly half of the global population relies on non-sewered sanitation. In non-sewered settings, urban drainage channels have been used for poliovirus environmental surveillance, but their potential for a multi-pathogen WES with spatially defined catchments, and comparability to sewer-based surveillance, remains under-explored. Methods: Ten drainage channel sampling points with delineated micro-catchments (0.95-3.83 km2 with 12,885-44,146 people) were selected within Kampala. A total of 255 drainage channel and 54 wastewater treatment plant (WWTP) influent samples were collected during two campaigns in March and September-October 2025. A multi-target panel was quantified by digital PCR, including enteric viruses (Norovirus GI and GII, Rotavirus), respiratory viruses (SARS-CoV-2, Influenza A and B viruses, Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)), non-O1/O139 Vibrio cholerae (V. cholerae), and Pepper Mild Mottle Virus (PMMoV) as a fecal indicator. Findings: Enteric viruses, non-O1/O139 V. cholerae, and PMMoV were consistently detected across all drainage channels and WWTP influents. Concentrations were generally lower in drainage than WWTP influents, except for non-O1/O139 V. When normalized by PMMoV, concentrations across most drainage channels were comparable to WWTP influents, although comparability varied by target and location. Both concentrations and PMMoV-normalized concentrations varied across sampling locations. Trends between campaigns varied by pathogen target and were not explained by any single micro-catchment characteristic. Influenza A virus was the most frequently detected respiratory virus (8-23% in drainage channels; 3-10% in WWTP influents), while SARS-CoV-2, Influenza B, and RSV were rarely detected. Interpretation: PMMoV-normalized concentrations across most micro-catchments were comparable to WWTP influents, with spatial heterogeneity implying neighborhood-level differences in disease prevalence. Catchment-delineated drainage surveillance has potential to offer spatially resolved public health information in non-sewered settings comparable to sewer-based wastewater monitoring. Funding: Eawag Discretionary Funding
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the potential of urban drainage channels for multi-pathogen wastewater and environmental surveillance in non-sewered settings, addressing the gap in understanding their comparability to sewer-based systems.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Kampala, Uganda, during two campaigns in March and September-October 2025, involving 10 drainage channel sampling points with defined micro-catchments and 255 drainage channel samples alongside 54 wastewater treatment plant influent samples.
Core methods
A multi-target panel was quantified using digital PCR, including enteric viruses, respiratory viruses such as influenza A and B, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV, as well as non-O1/O139 Vibrio cholerae and PMMoV as a fecal indicator.
Main results
Enteric viruses, non-O1/O139 V. cholerae, and PMMoV were consistently detected across all drainage channels and WWTP influents. Influenza A virus was the most frequently detected respiratory virus, with 8-23% in drainage channels and 3-10% in WWTP influents, while SARS-CoV-2, Influenza B, and RSV were rarely detected.
Interpretation and novelty
PMMoV-normalized concentrations across most micro-catchments were comparable to WWTP influents, suggesting that drainage-based surveillance can provide spatially resolved public health information in non-sewered settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
Catchment-delineated drainage surveillance has potential to offer spatially resolved public health information in non-sewered settings comparable to sewer-based wastewater monitoring, which could inform targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results varied across sampling locations and campaigns, and trends were not explained by any single micro-catchment characteristic, indicating potential limitations in spatial generalizability and the need for further validation.
