中国山西省流感亚型的差异气象驱动因素

Differential meteorological drivers of influenza subtypes in Shanxi Province, China
作者： Rong Zhang, Yifei Ning, Jiaming Guo, Hongmei Yu, Rui Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查中国山西省流感亚型的差异气象驱动因素,因为流感表现出明显的季节和地理异质性,表明气象因素在流感 传播 中发挥着关键的调节作用。
设计与对象
该研究使用了2009-2024年山西省实验室确认的流感监测数据和同期气象数据,不包括COVID-19非药物干预期间。该研究重点关注流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒 和 B 病毒,研究期间共发现 29,246 例病例。
核心方法
采用多步骤分析方法,包括两阶段随机森林来识别关键气象变量,单变量分析来确定最佳滞后结构,以及多元逻辑回归模型来识别独立效应。还进行了联合效应分析,探讨气象因素之间的协同作用和拮抗作用。
主要结果
共确诊流感实验室确诊病例29246例,其中甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒、B型分别占28.7%、39.6%、31.7%。绝对湿度成为所有亚型中最强大的保护因素。3个亚型表现出不同的气象驱动模式,甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09对低温、低湿、大风速敏感;甲型H3N2流感病毒 主要受较高的太阳辐射和温度调节;B与湿度-压力系统密切相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,流感亚型对气象因素表现出不同的反应,这些因素之间存在复杂的协同和拮抗作用,凸显了气象预警系统中亚型特异性模型的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为开发更准确的流感气象预警模型提供信息,该模型可以通过考虑特定亚型的气象驱动因素来改善 监测 和公共卫生准备工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究排除了 COVID-19 非药物干预期间,这可能影响了流感 传播 模式。证据是中等的,因为研究结果是基于观察数据和统计分析而不是实验干预。

Differential meteorological drivers of influenza subtypes in Shanxi Province, China

Authors: Rong Zhang, Yifei Ning, Jiaming Guo, Hongmei Yu, Rui Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Methods Based on laboratory-confirmed influenza surveillance data and concurrent meteorological data from Shanxi Province during 2009–2024 (excluding the period of COVID-19 non-pharmaceutical interventions), a multi-step analytical approach was employed to systematically evaluate the meteorological drivers of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B viruses. Results A total of 29,246 laboratory-confirmed influenza cases were identified during the study period, with A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B accounting for 28.7%, 39.6%, and 31.7%, respectively. These findings suggest that the development of influenza meteorological early warning models should consider subtype specificity and multi-factor interactions, rather than relying on single universal indicators. (As this study is not a clinical trial.) Abbreviations - WHO: - World Health Organization - ILI : - Influenza-like illness - RT-PCR : - Reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction - NPIs: - Non-pharmaceutical interventions - ECMWF: - European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts - RF: - Random forest - AUC-ROC : - Area under the receiver operating characteristic curve - RMSE: - Root mean square error - AIC: - Akaike information criterion - ORs: - Odds ratios - CIs: - Confidence intervals - FDR: - False discovery rate - VIFs: - Variance inflation factors - BIC: - Bayesian Information Criterion - CV: - Coefficient of variation Acknowledgements Not applicable. Abstract Background Influenza exhibits pronounced seasonal and geographical heterogeneity, suggesting that meteorological factors play a key regulatory role in influenza transmission. However, whether different influenza subtypes possess distinct meteorological drivers remains unclear, and systematic evidence from temperate continental monsoon climate regions...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the differential meteorological drivers of influenza subtypes in Shanxi Province, China, as influenza exhibits pronounced seasonal and geographical heterogeneity, suggesting that meteorological factors play a key regulatory role in influenza transmission.
Design and population
The study used laboratory-confirmed influenza surveillance data and concurrent meteorological data from Shanxi Province during 2009–2024, excluding the period of COVID-19 non-pharmaceutical interventions. It focused on influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B viruses, with a total of 29,246 cases identified during the study period.
Core methods
A multi-step analytical approach was employed, including a two-stage random forest to identify key meteorological variables, univariate analysis to determine optimal lag structures, and multivariate logistic regression models to identify independent effects. Joint effect analysis was also conducted to explore synergistic and antagonistic effects among meteorological factors.
Main results
A total of 29,246 laboratory-confirmed influenza cases were identified, with A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B accounting for 28.7%, 39.6%, and 31.7%, respectively. Absolute humidity emerged as the most robust protective factor across all subtypes. The three subtypes exhibited distinct meteorological driver patterns, with A(H1N1)pdm09 sensitive to low temperature, low humidity, and high wind speed; A(H3N2) primarily regulated by higher solar radiation and temperature; and B closely associated with the humidity‒pressure system.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that influenza subtypes exhibit differential responses to meteorological factors, with complex synergistic and antagonistic effects among these factors, highlighting the need for subtype-specific models in meteorological early warning systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform the development of more accurate influenza meteorological early warning models, which could improve surveillance and public health preparedness by accounting for subtype-specific meteorological drivers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study excluded the period of COVID-19 non-pharmaceutical interventions, which may have affected influenza transmission patterns. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on observational data and statistical analysis rather than experimental intervention.

多重 PCR 揭示呼吸道病原体的年龄和季节模式及其联合检测

Age and seasonal patterns of respiratory pathogens and their codetections revealed by multiplex PCR
作者： Yasemin Genç Bahçe, Asiye Bıçakcıgil, Ferhat Zengin, Ömer Acer, İsmail Selçuk Aygar
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查呼吸道病原体(特别是流感病毒)的年龄和季节模式,以及它们在大流行后环境中的共同检测,因为呼吸道感染由于其病因多样且临床表现重叠,仍然是一个重要的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究分析了 2024 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月土耳其 Siirt 培训和研究医院临床疑似 RTI 患者的连续鼻咽拭子标本。患者被分为预定义的年龄组。
核心方法
该研究使用多重 RT-qPCR 检测呼吸道病原体,并进行多变量逻辑回归分析,以确定与病原体检测相关的独立因素,包括年龄和季节。
主要结果
Among 1,666 specimens, respiratory pathogens were detected in 1,166 (70.0%). Bacterial-only detection accounted for 473 (40.6%), viral-only detection for 395 (33.9%), and viral–bacterial co-detection for 298 (25.6%) of positive specimens. Children had significantly higher odds of pathogen detection than adults (aOR 2.27, 95% CI 1.72–2.99; p < 0.001). Respiratory pathogen detection was significantly lower during summer (aOR 0.58, 95% CI 0.43–0.78; p < 0.001) and autumn (aOR 0.57, 95% CI 0.43–0.75; p < 0.001) compared to winter.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,呼吸道病原体检测存在明显的年龄相关和季节性模式,包括频繁的病毒-细菌联合检测,这提供了对这些病原体的 流行病学 的更全面的了解。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为改进的 监测 策略提供信息,增强诊断方法,并指导有针对性的公共卫生干预措施,以解决呼吸道感染的季节性变化和特定年龄风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对匿名数据的依赖可能会限制临床解释的深度,并且由于缺乏前瞻性数据和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Age and seasonal patterns of respiratory pathogens and their codetections revealed by multiplex PCR

Authors: Yasemin Genç Bahçe, Asiye Bıçakcıgil, Ferhat Zengin, Ömer Acer, İsmail Selçuk Aygar
Original abstract (English)
This retrospective cross-sectional study included consecutive nasopharyngeal swab specimens obtained from patients with clinically suspected RTIs who underwent multiplex RT-qPCR testing at Siirt Training and Research Hospital, Türkiye, between January 2024 and December 2025. Because multiplex RT-qPCR detects microbial nucleic acids rather than confirming active infection, bacterial detections—particularly for common nasopharyngeal colonizers—should be interpreted together with clinical findings and complementary microbiological investigations. Ethics approval and consent to participate This study was approved by the Siirt University Non-Interventional Clinical Research Ethics Committee (Decision No: 2026/01/03/4 Decision Date: 02.03.2026). Due to the retrospective design of the study and the use of anonymized data, the requirement for informed consent to participate was waived by the Siirt University Non-Interventional Clinical Research Ethics Committee. This applies to all patients and all patient data included in the study. The study was conducted in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki. Abstract Respiratory tract infections (RTIs) remain a major cause of morbidity worldwide owing to their diverse etiol...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the age and seasonal patterns of respiratory pathogens, particularly influenza viruses, and their co-detections in a post-pandemic setting, as RTIs remain a significant public health issue due to their diverse etiologies and overlapping clinical presentations.
Design and population
This retrospective cross-sectional study analyzed consecutive nasopharyngeal swab specimens from patients with clinically suspected RTIs at Siirt Training and Research Hospital in Türkiye between January 2024 and December 2025. Patients were categorized into predefined age groups.
Core methods
The study used multiplex RT-qPCR to detect respiratory pathogens and performed multivariable logistic regression analysis to identify independent factors associated with pathogen detection, including age and season.
Main results
Among 1,666 specimens, respiratory pathogens were detected in 1,166 (70.0%). Bacterial-only detection accounted for 473 (40.6%), viral-only detection for 395 (33.9%), and viral–bacterial co-detection for 298 (25.6%) of positive specimens. Children had significantly higher odds of pathogen detection than adults (aOR 2.27, 95% CI 1.72–2.99; p < 0.001). Respiratory pathogen detection was significantly lower during summer (aOR 0.58, 95% CI 0.43–0.78; p < 0.001) and autumn (aOR 0.57, 95% CI 0.43–0.75; p < 0.001) compared to winter.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate distinct age-related and seasonal patterns in respiratory pathogen detection, including frequent viral–bacterial co-detection, which provides a more comprehensive understanding of the epidemiology of these pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform improved surveillance strategies, enhance diagnostic approaches, and guide targeted public health interventions to address seasonal variations and age-specific risks in respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design and reliance on anonymized data may limit the depth of clinical interpretation, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of prospective data and potential confounding factors.

超越病毒载量:病毒免疫动力学和年龄依赖性流感严重程度的机制模型

Beyond Viral Load: A Mechanistic Model of Virus-Immune Dynamics and Age-Dependent Influenza Severity
作者： Johnson, R., Neujahr, H., Blanco, R., Hernandez Vargas, E. A.
摘要中文翻译流感感染是由病毒复制、宿主 免疫应答 和临床症状的出现之间紧密耦合的相互作用引起的。虽然数学模型已经广泛描述了病毒和免疫动力学的特征,但免疫活动和疾病严重程度之间的机制联系仍然知之甚少。在这里,我们开发了一个综合的 宿主 内建模框架,该框架通过统一的动力系统明确连接感染动态、免疫应答 和症状表现。使用鼠流感数据,我们将关键的免疫成分与症状进展的机制表示结合起来,通过 宿主 体重减轻进行量化。我们的分析确定了炎症信号传导,特别是 TNF 介导的途径,作为将免疫活动与症状严重程度联系起来的中心驱动因素。重要的是,我们证明,年龄依赖性的免疫调节改变重塑了这种耦合:尽管病毒动力学相当,但衰老的 宿主 表现出延长的炎症反应,从而放大和维持症状负担。这些结果强调,疾病的严重程度不能仅从病毒载量来推断,而是从免疫调节和 宿主 生理学之间的动态相互作用中得出。该框架为了解特定年龄的发病率提供了定量基础,并为设计针对免疫介导的病理学而不是单独的病毒复制的干预措施奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了为什么当前的流感严重程度模型严重依赖病毒载量,无法全面了解疾病结果。它凸显了在理解 免疫应答 如何机械地驱动病毒复制之外的症状严重程度方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
作者开发了一个综合的 宿主 内建模框架,将感染动态、免疫应答 和症状进展联系起来。该研究使用鼠流感数据来量化 宿主 体重减轻,作为症状严重程度的衡量标准。
核心方法
该框架整合了关键的免疫成分和症状进展的机制表示,其中炎症信号传导(尤其是 TNF 介导的途径)被认为是将免疫活动与症状严重程度联系起来的核心。该模型通过统一的动力系统明确地将病毒复制、免疫应答 和临床症状联系起来。
主要结果
分析表明,炎症信号传导,尤其是 TNF 介导的途径,驱动免疫活动与症状严重程度之间的耦合。尽管病毒动力学相当,但老年 宿主 表现出长时间的炎症反应,从而放大并维持症状负担。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明疾病的严重程度不能仅从病毒载量来推断,而是从免疫调节和 宿主 生理学之间的动态相互作用中得出。新颖之处在于通过机制模型将免疫介导的病理学与症状表现明确联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
该框架为了解流感的年龄特异性发病率提供了定量基础,并表明针对免疫介导的病理学而不是病毒复制的干预措施可能更有效地降低疾病的严重程度。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于小鼠数据和仅摘要的报告,这限制了对人类群体的普遍适用性。由于依赖单一模型生物且缺乏人类验证,证据的强度中等。

Beyond Viral Load: A Mechanistic Model of Virus-Immune Dynamics and Age-Dependent Influenza Severity

Authors: Johnson, R., Neujahr, H., Blanco, R., Hernandez Vargas, E. A.
Original abstract (English)
Influenza infection results from tightly coupled interactions between viral replication, host immune responses, and the emergence of clinical symptoms. While mathematical models have extensively characterized viral and immune dynamics, the mechanistic link between immune activity and disease severity remains poorly understood. Here, we develop an integrative, within-host modeling framework that explicitly connects infection dynamics, immune responses, and symptom manifestation through a unified dynamical system. Using murine influenza data, we incorporate key immune components alongside a mechanistic representation of symptom progression, quantified via host weight loss.Our analysis identifies inflammatory signaling, particularly TNF- mediated pathways, as a central driver linking immune activity to symptom severity. Importantly, we demonstrate that age-dependent alterations in immune regulation reshape this coupling: aged hosts exhibit prolonged inflammatory responses that amplify and sustain symptom burden despite comparable viral kinetics. These results highlight that disease severity cannot be inferred from viral load alone, but instead emerges from the dynamical interplay between immune regulation and host physiology. This framework provides a quantitative basis for understanding age-specific morbidity and offers a foundation for designing interventions that target immune-mediated pathology rather than viral replication alone.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why current models of influenza severity, which rely heavily on viral load, fail to capture the full picture of disease outcomes. It highlights a knowledge gap in understanding how immune responses mechanistically drive symptom severity beyond viral replication.
Design and population
The authors developed an integrative within-host modeling framework that links infection dynamics, immune responses, and symptom progression. The study used murine influenza data to quantify host weight loss as a measure of symptom severity.
Core methods
The framework incorporated key immune components and a mechanistic representation of symptom progression, with inflammatory signaling—particularly TNF-mediated pathways—identified as central to linking immune activity to symptom severity. The model explicitly connected viral replication, immune responses, and clinical symptoms through a unified dynamical system.
Main results
The analysis showed that inflammatory signaling, especially TNF-mediated pathways, drives the coupling between immune activity and symptom severity. Aged hosts exhibited prolonged inflammatory responses that amplified and sustained symptom burden despite comparable viral kinetics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that disease severity cannot be inferred from viral load alone but instead emerges from the dynamic interplay between immune regulation and host physiology. The novelty lies in explicitly connecting immune-mediated pathology to symptom manifestation through a mechanistic model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework provides a quantitative basis for understanding age-specific morbidity in influenza and suggests that interventions targeting immune-mediated pathology, rather than viral replication, may be more effective for reducing disease severity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to murine data and an abstract-only report, which constrains generalizability to human populations. The strength of the evidence is moderate due to the reliance on a single model organism and the absence of human validation.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

2023-2025 年英国流感调查参与者中与 疫苗接种 对抗 季节性流感 和 SARS-CoV-2 相关的因素。

Factors associated with vaccination against Seasonal Influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in the UK among FluSurvey participants, 2023-2025.
作者： Lauren Remi Adams, Conall Watson, Rebecca E Green, Gavin Dabrera, Adams, L. R., Green, R. E.
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 计划对于降低老年人的发病率和 死亡率 至关重要,但不同人群的吸收率仍然不平衡。我们的目的是在英国参与式 监测 系统 FluSurvey 中分析 疫苗接种 态度并检查 COVID-19/流感 疫苗接种 吸收的预测因子。我们分析了 2023-2024 年和 2024-2025 年秋冬季节活动中年龄≥65 岁、有资格参加 疫苗 的参与者的流感调查数据。描述性分析检查了自我报告的对流感 疫苗接种 的态度。Logistic 回归研究了每个季节与流感和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 摄入量相关的因素(年龄、性别、社会经济地位、教育、就业、交通、吸烟和慢性病),并调整了混杂因素。属于风险群体和降低流感风险是经常报告的流感 疫苗接种 动机,而建立自然免疫力以及对安全性和不良反应的担忧是经常报告的障碍。接种了 COVID-19 疫苗的个体更有可能感染流感 疫苗接种 (aOR2023-2024=13.90 [9.28-21.17];aOR2024-2025=8.54 [5.82-12.60]),反之亦然 (aOR2023-2024=13.91 [9.30-21.19];aOR2024-2025=8.52[5.81-12.58])。较低的教育程度与较低的 COVID-19 疫苗接种 几率相关(aOR2023-2024=0.59 [0.45-0.78],aOR2024-2025:0.56 [0.39-0.79])。其他结果较弱或表现出随季节变化的变化。我们的研究结果强调了流感调查 队列研究 中最近对流感和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 的态度和障碍,这可能为提高 疫苗接种 在人群中覆盖率的方法提供参考。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 季节性流感 病毒,特别研究了 2023-2024 年和 2024-2025 年秋冬季运动期间英国流感调查参与者中与 季节性流感 和 SARS-CoV-2 相关的 疫苗接种 相关因素。核心问题是分析 疫苗接种 态度并确定两种病原体 疫苗 摄取的预测因子。
证据基础与方法
该综述分析了年龄≥65岁且符合 疫苗 资格的参与者的流感调查数据。使用逻辑回归来检查与流感和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 摄取相关的因素(年龄、性别、社会经济地位、教育、就业、交通、吸烟和慢性病),并调整混杂因素。进行描述性分析以探讨自我报告的对流感 疫苗接种 的态度。
共识与结论
经常报道的流感 疫苗接种 的动机是属于风险群体和降低流感风险。对安全性和不良反应的担忧是常见的障碍。接种了 COVID-19 疫苗的个体更有可能感染流感 疫苗接种,反之亦然,表明 疫苗 摄取这两种病原体之间存在潜在关联。
争议与缺口
较低的教育程度与较低的 COVID-19 疫苗接种 几率相关。其他结果较弱或随季节变化而变化,表明研究结果的一致性有限。该综述没有提供有关目标与相关实体之间的生物学或分类学关系的详细信息,也没有解决理解这些关系方面的具体差距。
科研与实践启示
研究结果强调了流感调查 队列研究 中最近对流感和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 的态度和障碍,这可能为提高 疫苗接种 在人群中覆盖率的策略提供参考。需要进一步的研究来探索 疫苗 吸收的季节性变化,并解决在理解影响 疫苗接种 行为的因素方面的证据差距。

Factors associated with vaccination against Seasonal Influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in the UK among FluSurvey participants, 2023-2025.

Authors: Lauren Remi Adams, Conall Watson, Rebecca E Green, Gavin Dabrera, Adams, L. R., Green, R. E.
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal Influenza and COVID-19 vaccination programmes are critical for reducing morbidity and mortality in older adults, yet uptake remains uneven across populations. We aimed to profile vaccination attitudes and examine predictors of COVID-19/influenza vaccination uptake among a UK participatory surveillance system - FluSurvey. We analysed FluSurvey data from participants aged [≥]65 years who were eligible for both vaccines in the 2023-2024 and 2024-2025 Autumn - Winter seasonal campaigns. Descriptive analyses examined self-reported attitudes to influenza vaccination. Logistic regression examined factors (age, sex, socioeconomic status, education, employment, transport, smoking and chronic conditions) associated with influenza and COVID-19 vaccination uptake in each season, adjusting for confounders. Belonging to a risk group and reducing risk of influenza were frequently reported motivations for influenza vaccination, while building natural immunity and concerns around safety and adverse effects were frequently reported barriers. Individuals vaccinated against COVID-19 were more likely to receive an influenza vaccination (aOR2023-2024=13.90 [9.28-21.17]; aOR2024-2025=8.54 [5.82-12.60]), and vice-versa (aOR2023-2024=13.91 [9.30-21.19]; aOR2024-2025=8.52 [5.81-12.58]). Lower educational attainment was associated with lower odds of COVID-19 vaccination (aOR2023-2024=0.59 [0.45-0.78], aOR2024-2025: 0.56 [0.39-0.79]). Other results were weaker or demonstrated variation by season. Our findings highlight recent attitudes and barriers to influenza and COVID-19 vaccination among the FluSurvey cohort, which may inform approaches to improve vaccination coverage in the population.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on Seasonal Influenza viruses, specifically examining factors associated with vaccination against Seasonal Influenza and SARS-CoV-2 in the UK among FluSurvey participants during the 2023-2024 and 2024-2025 Autumn-Winter campaigns. The central question is to profile vaccination attitudes and identify predictors of vaccine uptake for both pathogens.
Evidence base and method
The review analyzed FluSurvey data from participants aged ≥65 years who were eligible for both vaccines. Logistic regression was used to examine factors (age, sex, socioeconomic status, education, employment, transport, smoking, and chronic conditions) associated with influenza and COVID-19 vaccination uptake, adjusting for confounders. Descriptive analyses were conducted to explore self-reported attitudes toward influenza vaccination.
Consensus and conclusions
Belonging to a risk group and reducing the risk of influenza were frequently reported motivations for influenza vaccination. Concerns about safety and adverse effects were common barriers. Individuals vaccinated against COVID-19 were more likely to receive an influenza vaccination, and vice versa, indicating a potential association between vaccine uptake for these two pathogens.
Controversies and gaps
Lower educational attainment was associated with lower odds of COVID-19 vaccination. Other results were weaker or showed variation by season, suggesting limited consistency in findings. The review does not provide detailed information on the biological or taxonomic relationships between the target and related entities, nor does it address specific gaps in understanding these relationships.
Research and practice implications
The findings highlight recent attitudes and barriers to influenza and COVID-19 vaccination among the FluSurvey cohort, which may inform strategies to improve vaccination coverage in the population. Further research is needed to explore the seasonal variations in vaccine uptake and to address the evidence gaps in understanding the factors influencing vaccination behavior.

破译乙型流感病毒衍生肽及其通过 HLA I 类分子的呈递。

Deciphering influenza B virus-derived peptides and their presentation by HLA class I molecules.
作者： Janesha C Maddumage, Georgia R Dow, Lawton D Murdolo, Samuel Liwei Leong, Demetra S M Chatzileontiadou, Emma J Grant, Stephanie Gras
摘要中文翻译尽管流感 疫苗 已广泛使用,但流感仍然每年导致显着的发病率和 死亡率。疫苗 通常会诱导体液介导的针对快速突变表面糖蛋白的保护,因此需要每年更新和施用它们。相比之下,可以控制和清除病毒感染的CD8+ T细胞可以识别更保守的病毒表位。因此,人们对了解 CD8+ T 细胞对流感病毒的反应有很大兴趣,以开发未来的 疫苗 和治疗方法。尽管甲型流感病毒 (FLUAV) 和甲型流感病毒 (FLUBV) 在人类中共同传播,并且都导致季节性流行,但有关 CD8+ T 细胞对 FLUBV 反应的数据有限。这种知识差距涵盖了免疫学和分子学方面的见解。在本综述中,我们总结了目前对 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位在细胞和分子水平上的了解,并与 FLUAV 进行了比较。总的来说,这凸显了有关 FLUBV 的可用数据有限,尽管它在人类流感感染中发挥着重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了人类 季节性流感 B 病毒 (FLUBV),重点是 CD8+ T 细胞对保守表位的反应,与甲型流感病毒 (FLUAV) 进行对比,并解决了这些反应如何为未来的 疫苗 和治疗提供信息的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了细胞和分子水平上 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位的最新知识,明确比较了 FLUBV 与 FLUAV,并强调了有关 FLUBV 的可用数据有限,尽管它在人类流感感染中发挥着作用。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,CD8+ T 细胞比体液反应识别更保守的病毒表位,从而提供了更广泛保护的潜力,但指出与 FLUAV 相比,FLUBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞表位数据仍然稀疏。
争议与缺口
该综述指出了关于 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位的重大知识差距,涵盖免疫学和分子见解,并强调在这方面缺乏 FLUBV 和 FLUAV 之间的比较数据。
科研与实践启示
该综述优先研究表征 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位及其 HLA I 类分子的呈递,旨在指导 疫苗 和利用保守 T 细胞反应的疗法的开发。

Deciphering influenza B virus-derived peptides and their presentation by HLA class I molecules.

Authors: Janesha C Maddumage, Georgia R Dow, Lawton D Murdolo, Samuel Liwei Leong, Demetra S M Chatzileontiadou, Emma J Grant, Stephanie Gras
Original abstract (English)
Despite influenza vaccines being widely available, influenza still causes significant morbidity and mortality annually. Vaccines typically induce humoral-mediated protection against rapidly mutating surface glycoproteins, necessitating that they be updated and administered each year. In contrast, CD8+ T cells, which can control and clear viral infections, can recognise more conserved viral epitopes. Therefore, there is considerable interest in understanding CD8+ T cell responses to influenza virus for the development of future vaccines and therapeutics. Although Alphainfluenzavirus influenzae (FLUAV) and Betainfluenzavirus influenzae (FLUBV) co-circulate in humans and both contribute to seasonal epidemics, there is limited data regarding CD8+ T cell responses to FLUBV. This knowledge gap spans both immunological and molecular insights. In the present review, we summarise the current knowledge of FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes at both cellular and molecular levels, in comparison with FLUAV. Collectively, this highlights the limited data available on FLUBV, despite its significant role in human influenza infections.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human seasonal influenza B virus (FLUBV) with a focus on CD8+ T cell responses to conserved epitopes, contrasting with influenza A virus (FLUAV) and addressing the central question of how these responses could inform future vaccines and therapeutics.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes current knowledge on FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes at cellular and molecular levels, explicitly comparing FLUBV to FLUAV, and highlights the limited data available on FLUBV despite its role in human influenza infections.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that CD8+ T cells recognize more conserved viral epitopes than humoral responses, offering potential for broader protection, but notes that FLUBV-specific CD8+ T cell epitope data remain sparse compared to FLUAV.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a significant knowledge gap regarding FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes, spanning both immunological and molecular insights, and emphasizes the lack of comparative data between FLUBV and FLUAV in this context.
Research and practice implications
The review prioritizes research to characterize FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes and their presentation by HLA class I molecules, aiming to guide the development of vaccines and therapeutics that leverage conserved T cell responses.

流感感染的“代谢增殖”机制和治疗靶点

Mechanisms of ‘metabolizeaccretion’ and therapeutic targets in influenza infection
作者： Kexin Wang, Yuepeng Li, Xiaohong Chen, Zifeng Yang, Wang K, Li Y, Chen X, Yang Z.
摘要中文翻译流感病毒感染仍然是全球急性呼吸道疾病的主要原因,对传统 抗病毒药物 疗法的耐药性不断增加,凸显了对新治疗策略的需求。这篇综述介绍了代谢增加的概念,这是一种由代谢重编程驱动的自我强化的病理级联反应,其特点是三个进展阶段:触发、放大和持续。在流感感染期间,病毒诱导的葡萄糖、脂质、氨基酸和嘌呤代谢的改变导致代谢中间体的异常积累,这与细胞能量稳态破坏、炎症和免疫应答改变以及可能稳定病理表型的潜在表观遗传修饰有关。这些过程共同影响病毒复制、免疫逃避以及严重呼吸道损伤的进展。我们进一步讨论针对关键代谢酶、累积代谢物、基因表达调节和破坏代谢积累的特定受体的潜在治疗干预措施。最后,我们强调多靶点方法和精准医学策略在解决肺部微环境代谢异质性方面的重要性,为 治疗 以及流感和相关呼吸系统疾病的管理提供新的见解。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题

证据基础与方法
这篇综述介绍了代谢增加的概念,这是一种由代谢重编程驱动的自我强化的病理级联反应,其特点是三个进展阶段:触发、放大和持续。
共识与结论
在流感感染期间,病毒诱导的葡萄糖、脂质、氨基酸和嘌呤代谢的改变导致代谢中间体的异常积累,这与细胞能量稳态破坏、炎症和免疫应答改变以及可能稳定病理表型的潜在表观遗传修饰有关。
争议与缺口
这些过程共同影响病毒复制、免疫逃避以及严重呼吸道损伤的进展。
科研与实践启示
我们进一步讨论针对关键代谢酶、累积代谢物、基因表达调节和破坏代谢积累的特定受体的潜在治疗干预措施。

Mechanisms of ‘metabolizeaccretion’ and therapeutic targets in influenza infection

Authors: Kexin Wang, Yuepeng Li, Xiaohong Chen, Zifeng Yang, Wang K, Li Y, Chen X, Yang Z.
Original abstract (English)
Influenza virus infection remains a leading cause of acute respiratory illness worldwide, with increasing resistance to conventional antiviral therapies highlighting the need for novel therapeutic strategies. This review introduces the concept of metabolizeaccretion, a self-reinforcing pathological cascade driven by metabolic reprogramming and characterized by three progressive stages: trigger, amplification, and persistence. During influenza infection, viral-induced alterations in glucose, lipid, amino acid, and purine metabolism lead to the aberrant accumulation of metabolic intermediates, which are associated with disrupted cellular energy homeostasis, altered inflammatory and immune responses, and potential epigenetic modifications that may stabilize pathological phenotypes. These processes collectively effect viral replication, immune evasion, and progression to severe respiratory damage. We further discuss potential therapeutic interventions targeting key metabolic enzymes, accumulated metabolites, gene expression regulation, and specific receptors to disrupt metabolizeaccretion. Finally, we emphasize the importance of multi-target approaches and precision medicine strategies to address metabolic heterogeneity in lung microenvironments, offering new insights for the treatment and management of influenza and related respiratory diseases.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Influenza virus infection remains a leading cause of acute respiratory illness worldwide, with increasing resistance to conventional antiviral therapies highlighting the need for novel therapeutic strategies.
Evidence base and method
This review introduces the concept of metabolizeaccretion, a self-reinforcing pathological cascade driven by metabolic reprogramming and characterized by three progressive stages: trigger, amplification, and persistence.
Consensus and conclusions
During influenza infection, viral-induced alterations in glucose, lipid, amino acid, and purine metabolism lead to the aberrant accumulation of metabolic intermediates, which are associated with disrupted cellular energy homeostasis, altered inflammatory and immune responses, and potential epigenetic modifications that may stabilize pathological phenotypes.
Controversies and gaps
These processes collectively effect viral replication, immune evasion, and progression to severe respiratory damage.
Research and practice implications
We further discuss potential therapeutic interventions targeting key metabolic enzymes, accumulated metabolites, gene expression regulation, and specific receptors to disrupt metabolizeaccretion.

流感血凝素界面抗体的一些暴露。

Some Exposure for Influenza Hemagglutinin Interface Antibodies.
作者： Kevin R. McCarthy
摘要中文翻译摘要:流感病毒对人类健康构成持续威胁。目前的 疫苗 通常对季节性病毒感染具有低至中度的保护作用,而对流行性病毒的保护作用几乎没有甚至没有。需要改进 疫苗。这些 疫苗 将需要可靠地引发抗体到具有广泛保护性的保守位点。实现这一目标的一种策略是设计免疫原,该免疫原呈现出基因相似的抗体所针对的表位,人类具有产生这些表位的遗传能力,并且可能存在于人类初始 B 细胞库中。在最近描述的广泛结合的表位中,保护性抗体是蛋白质-蛋白质界面表面上的抗体。本综述将重点描述其中许多位点以及参与这些位点的抗体的遗传特征。界面表位具有免疫原性,并且这些位点的抗体可能存在于流感感染或 疫苗接种 后的人类库中。

Some Exposure for Influenza Hemagglutinin Interface Antibodies.

Authors: Kevin R. McCarthy
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Influenza viruses are a constant threat to human health. Current vaccines often confer low to moderate protection against infection with seasonal viruses and little‐to‐no protection against pandemic viruses. Improved vaccines are needed. These vaccines will need to reliably elicit antibodies to conserved sites that are broadly protective. One strategy to do so is to design immunogens that present epitopes that are targeted by antibodies that are genetically similar, that humans have the genetic capacity to produce, and that are likely present in human naive B cell repertoires. Among the more recently described epitopes for broadly binding, protective antibodies are those on surfaces at protein–protein interfaces. This review will focus on describing many of these sites and the genetic features of antibodies that engage them. Interface epitopes are immunogenic, and antibodies to these sites are likely present in human repertoires following influenza infection or vaccination.

This review will focus on describing many of these sites and the genetic features of antibodies that engage them.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
Among the more recently described epitopes for broadly binding, protective antibodies are those on surfaces at protein–protein interfaces.
Research and practice implications
Interface epitopes are immunogenic, and antibodies to these sites are likely present in human repertoires following influenza infection or vaccination.

基于 NS 片段的报告甲型流感病毒:工程策略、应用和局限性

NS-segment-based reporter influenza A viruses: engineering strategy, applications, and limitations
作者： Ramya S. Barre, Himadri Nath, Aitor Nogales, Elsayed M. Abdelwhab, Ahmed M. Elsayed, Luis Martinez-Sobrido
摘要中文翻译摘要 表达报告基因的重组甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 已成为研究病毒感染、宿主 与病原体相互作用以及鉴定和表征 IAV 感染 治疗 预防和/或治疗对策的强大工具。通过将荧光、重组酶或荧光素酶蛋白等报告基因整合到病毒 基因组 中,这些基因工程重组 IAV 能够实现体外、离体和体内感染的实时可视化和定量,以检测感染细胞或感染动物模型中 IAV 的存在。在从病毒 基因组 表达报告基因的各种方法中,荧光或萤光素酶蛋白与非结构 1 (NS1) 蛋白 C 末端的融合已成功用于生成具有复制能力、表达报告基因的 IAV。这篇小综述总结了用于生成 IAV 表达报告基因的策略,特别是来自非结构 (NS) 病毒片段 8 的策略,以及它们在实时跟踪病毒复制和 发病机制 中的用途。这些具有复制能力、表达报告基因的 IAV 还为新型抗 IAV 疗法的高通量筛选 (HTS) 提供了有效的工具。我们还讨论了当前表达荧光和荧光素酶报告基因的 IAV 在培养细胞和/或动物模型中追踪病毒感染的优点和局限性,并为这个快速发展的领域的未来方向提供了展望。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是开发和使用基于 NS 片段的报告甲型流感病毒来研究病毒感染、宿主 与病原体的相互作用以及 IAV 感染对策的确定。目标实体包括季节性人类流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒、B/Victoria 和 B/Yamagata 病毒。核心问题是关于这些表达报告基因的病毒的工程策略和应用。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一篇小型综述,总结了用于生成 IAV 表达报告基因的策略,特别是来自非结构 (NS) 病毒片段 8。它讨论了将荧光、重组酶或荧光素酶蛋白等报告基因整合到病毒 基因组 中,以实现体外、离体和体内感染的实时可视化和定量。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
该综述强调,荧光或萤光素酶蛋白与 NS1 蛋白 C 端的融合已成功用于生成具有复制能力、表达报告基因的 IAV。这些病毒可用于实时跟踪病毒复制和 发病机制,并且它们可作为新型抗 IAV 疗法的高通量筛选的有效工具。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,虽然报告者表达的 IAV 是有价值的工具,但仍讨论了它们的优点和局限性。然而,证据并未提供有关相互矛盾的发现、异质性或未解决的机制的具体细节。该审查也没有明确提及某些人群或地区的证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,未来的研究应侧重于提高表达报告基因的 IAV 在追踪培养细胞和动物模型中病毒感染方面的实用性。它还强调需要进一步探索这些病毒作为抗 IAV 疗法高通量筛选的工具。

NS-segment-based reporter influenza A viruses: engineering strategy, applications, and limitations

Authors: Ramya S. Barre, Himadri Nath, Aitor Nogales, Elsayed M. Abdelwhab, Ahmed M. Elsayed, Luis Martinez-Sobrido
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Reporter-expressing recombinant influenza A viruses (IAVs) have emerged as powerful tools for studying viral infection, host–pathogen interactions, and the identification and characterization of prophylactic and/or therapeutic countermeasures for the treatment of IAV infections. By incorporating a reporter gene, such as fluorescent, recombinase, or luciferase proteins, into the viral genome, these genetically engineered recombinant IAVs enable real-time visualization and quantification of infection in vitro , ex vivo , and in vivo to detect the presence of IAV in infected cells or animal models of infection. Among the various approaches to express reporter genes from the viral genome, fusion of fluorescent or luciferase proteins to the C-terminus of the non-structural 1 (NS1) protein has been successfully used to generate replication-competent reporter-expressing IAVs. This minireview summarizes the strategies used to generate IAV-expressing reporter genes, particularly from the non-structural (NS) viral segment 8, and their use in real-time tracking of viral replication and pathogenesis. These replication-competent reporter-expressing IAVs also provide an efficient tool for high-throughput screening (HTS) of novel anti-IAV therapeutics. We also discuss the advantages and limitations of current fluorescent and luciferase reporter-expressing IAVs to track viral infections in cultured cells and/or animal models, and provide perspectives on future directions in this rapidly advancing field.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the development and use of NS-segment-based reporter influenza A viruses to study viral infection, host–pathogen interactions, and the identification of countermeasures for IAV infections. The target entities include seasonal human influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), B/Victoria, and B/Yamagata viruses. The central question is about the engineering strategies and applications of these reporter-expressing viruses.
Evidence base and method
The review is a minireview that summarizes the strategies used to generate IAV-expressing reporter genes, particularly from the non-structural (NS) viral segment 8. It discusses the incorporation of reporter genes such as fluorescent, recombinase, or luciferase proteins into the viral genome to enable real-time visualization and quantification of infection in vitro, ex vivo, and in vivo. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus process.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that fusion of fluorescent or luciferase proteins to the C-terminus of the NS1 protein has been successfully used to generate replication-competent reporter-expressing IAVs. These viruses are useful for real-time tracking of viral replication and pathogenesis, and they serve as efficient tools for high-throughput screening of novel anti-IAV therapeutics.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while reporter-expressing IAVs are valuable tools, their advantages and limitations are discussed. However, the evidence does not provide specific details on conflicting findings, heterogeneity, or unresolved mechanisms. The review also does not explicitly mention evidence gaps in certain populations or regions.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on improving the utility of reporter-expressing IAVs for tracking viral infections in cultured cells and animal models. It also emphasizes the need for further exploration of these viruses as tools for high-throughput screening of anti-IAV therapeutics.

无孤岛的 监测:西非禽流感亟需单一健康数据

Surveillance without silos: a One Health data imperative for avian influenza in West Africa
作者： Emmanuel Musa, Francis Akpa, Godfred O. Boateng

Surveillance without silos: a One Health data imperative for avian influenza in West Africa

Authors: Emmanuel Musa, Francis Akpa, Godfred O. Boateng

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

随着印度应对季节性流感激增,世界卫生组织的 H5N1 警报变得更加紧迫 - ETV Bharat

WHO's H5N1 Alert Gains Urgency As India Battles Seasonal Flu Surge - ETV Bharat
新闻简报这篇文章强调了世界卫生组织 (WHO) 对 H5N1 病毒的高度警惕,由于 季节性流感 A(H1N1) 病例激增和禽流感 暴发 反复出现,这种病毒在印度变得越来越紧迫。世界卫生组织呼吁改进“One Health”监测,这突显印度需要对新出现的人畜共患流感威胁保持警惕。印度已报告在家禽和其他鸟类中感染禽流感 暴发,其中喀拉拉邦发生 11 起 H5N1 暴发 疫情,导致超过 54,000 只禽类死亡,并为预防措施而扑杀了数千只禽类。2020 年至 2024 年期间,印度报告了 1 例甲型禽流感 (H5N1) 死亡病例,2025 年报告了 2 例死亡病例。2026 年迄今为止,印度尚未报告确诊的人类 H5N1 病例。一般人群面临的风险仍然较低,但专家强调持续监测和 监测 的重要性,以防止潜在的 监测 感染人类。传染病专家马纳里·阿加瓦尔博士指出,H5N1主要通过与受感染鸟类或受污染环境的密切接触传播,不易在人与人之间传播。世界卫生组织强调,人类、动物和环境卫生当局之间需要进行有效协调,以便在人畜共患威胁升级之前发现并管理它们。
查看新闻五要素
时间
印度的担忧 对于印度来说,这种担忧不仅仅是理论上的。
地点与对象
印度,特别提到喀拉拉邦和新德里。
事件
世卫组织已发布有关 H5N1 病毒的警报,由于印度同时与 季节性流感 A(H1N1) 和禽流感 暴发 作斗争,该病毒变得越来越紧迫。该警报是基于 H5N1 在鸟类中的持续传播以及动物向人类 跨物种溢出 的可能性。
规模、影响与风险
过去曾在家禽和其他鸟类中报告过禽流感 暴发,其中喀拉拉邦发生 11 起 H5N1 暴发 事件,导致超过 54,000 只禽类死亡,并有数千只禽类被扑杀以防万一。根据世界卫生组织的数据,2020年至2024年期间,印度报告了1例甲型H5N1禽流感死亡病例,2025年报告了2例死亡病例。2026 年迄今为止,印度尚未报告人类 H5N1 确诊病例。H5N1 对普通人群的风险仍然很低,但持续的 监测 至关重要。
应对、状态与不确定性
世界卫生组织呼吁加强 One Health 监测,以便及早发现人畜共患病威胁。公共卫生专家强调实验室网络和预警系统的重要性。目前尚无H5N1病毒持续人传人的证据,总体公共卫生风险仍然较低。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

WHO's H5N1 Alert Gains Urgency As India Battles Seasonal Flu Surge - ETV Bharat

News Brief
The article highlights the World Health Organization's (WHO) heightened alert regarding the H5N1 virus, which is gaining urgency in India amid a surge in seasonal influenza A(H1N1) cases and recurring avian influenza outbreaks. The WHO's call for improved One Health surveillance underscores the need for India to remain vigilant against emerging zoonotic influenza threats. India has reported avian influenza outbreaks in poultry and other birds, with 11 H5N1 outbreaks in Kerala involving the deaths of over 54,000 birds and thousands more culled as a precaution. Between 2020-2024, India reported one death, and two deaths in 2025, due to avian influenza A(H5N1). No confirmed human H5N1 cases have been reported in India so far in 2026. The risk to the general population remains low, but experts stress the importance of continued monitoring and surveillance to prevent potential spillover into humans. Dr. Manali Agarwal, an infectious disease expert, noted that H5N1 primarily spreads through close contact with infected birds or contaminated environments and does not transmit easily from person to person. The WHO emphasized the need for effective coordination between human, animal, and environmental health authorities to detect and manage zoonotic threats before they escalate.
Original source text (English)
nepal floodsnepal flash floodsmohan bhagwat E हिं অ বাং ગુ ಕ മ म ଓ ਪੰ த తె ار National English हिंदी उत्तर प्रदेशउत्तराखंडछत्तीसगढ़झारखंडदिल्लीबिहारमध्य प्रदेशराजस्थानहरियाणाहिमाचल प्रदेश অসমীয়া বাংলা ગુજરાતી ಕನ್ನಡ മലയാളം मराठी ଓଡିଆ ਪੰਜਾਬੀ தமிழ் తెలుగు ఆంధ్రప్రదేశ్తెలంగాణ اردو Live NATIONAL WORLD SPORTS ENTERTAINMENT OPINION PHOTOS HEALTH STATE BUSINESS More Live ETV Bharat / bharat WHO's H5N1 Alert Gains Urgency As India Battles Seasonal Flu Surge WHO’s call for stronger One Health surveillance underscores the need for India to stay prepared for emerging zoonotic influenza threats, writes ETV Bharat’s Gautam Debroy. Representative picture (ETV Bharat) By ETV Bharat English Team Published : August 29, 2026 at 2:32 PM IST 5 Min Read New Delhi: The World Health Organization’s (WHO) latest appeal for Asia-Pacific countries to strengthen preparedness against highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1), assumes added significance for India, which is simultaneously reporting recurring bird flu outbreaks and witnessing a surge in seasonal influenza A (H1N1) cases. Over 150 influenza experts, laboratory scientists and public health officials participated in the recently held 19th Biregional Meeting of National Influenza Centres and Influenza Surveillance, jointly organised by WHO’s Southeast Asia and Western Pacific regional offices, from August 25 to August 27. The meeting examined ways to convert surveillance data into faster public health decisions, amid the continuing spread of H5N1 among birds, and the possibility of animal-to-human spillover. Concern For India For India, the concern is not merely theoretical. Avian flu outbreaks have been reported in poultry and other birds in the past. The Central government had reported avian influenza outbreaks in 2024 and 2025. More recently, 11 H5N1 outbreaks were reported in poultry farms in Kerala, involving the deaths of over 54,000 birds, with thousands more culled as a precaution. The risk of H5N1 to the general population remains low, but India’s recent history shows why sustained surveillance is essential. According to WHO data, between 2020-2024, India reported one death, and two deaths in 2025, due to the avian influenza A(H5N1). Genetic analysis indicated the virus belonged to clade 2.3.2.1a, a lineage circulating in the region. However, no confirmed human H5N1 case has been reported from India so far in 2026. Laboratory Networks, Early-Warning Systems Public health experts say the simultaneous experience of seasonal influenza and recurring avian outbreaks demonstrates the importance of strong laboratory networks and early-warning systems. “First, we need to understand what H5N1, also known as “avian influenza” or “bird flu”, is? It is a subtype of influenza virus that infects birds and mammals, including humans in rare instances. Infections in humans is mostly linked to close contact with infected birds and other animals and contaminated environments. This virus does not appear to transmit easily from person to person, and sustained human-to-human transmission has not been reported,” said Dr Manali Agarwal, infectious disease expert and medical microbiologist, to ETV Bharat on Saturday. Dr Manali Agarwal (ETV Bharat) According to Dr Agarwal, most human H5N1 cases are associated with the direct handling of infected poultry, slaughtering or preparing sick poultry for consumption, consumption of uncooked poultry products like raw blood, or close contact with live poultry. In bird-to-human transmission, the likely entry portal for the virus is via the respiratory tract, the gastrointestinal tract, or the conjunctiva. “Most patients with influenza H5N1 virus present with symptoms of fever, cough, and shortness of breath and radiological evidence of pneumonia. Frequently occurring non respiratory symptoms include diarrhea, vomiting, and abdominal pain. Surveillance and testing are important to understand which influenza viruses are circulating and their effect on human health. This is critical for pandemic preparedness and for protection of citizens,” Dr Agarwal, Ex-AIIMS, Delhi said. The WHO has stressed that effective One Health coordination — linking human, animal and environmental health authorities — is critical to detecting zoonotic threats before they spread further. The organisation also noted that sustained human-to-human transmission of H5N1 has not been reported, but influenza viruses can evolve, making continued monitoring crucial. H5N1: A Warning About A Different Influenza Threat Talking to ETV Bharat, another renowned health expert and past president of Asian Society of Emergency Medicine Dr Tamorish Kole said the call given by the global health watchdog should be seen not as a response to the H1N1 situation, but as a warning about a different influenza threat. “H1N1 is a seasonal human influenza virus that spreads efficiently from person to person, whereas H5N1 is primarily an avian influenza virus that can occasionally spill over into humans from infected birds or contaminated environments. For India, this is particularly important because of its large poultry sector, backyard poultry, live-bird markets, migratory-bird pathways and frequent human–animal interfaces,” said Dr Kole. Dr Tamorish Kole (ETV Bharat) H5N1 has already been detected in Indian birds, including the highly pathogenic clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, creating opportunities for human exposure and, potentially, viral adaptation. “The simultaneous presence of H1N1 therefore makes influenza surveillance more important, not less, with hospitals and laboratories needing to distinguish routine seasonal influenza from unusual influenza A infections. India should continue strengthening a One Health approach, integrating human, veterinary and environmental surveillance so that any zoonotic transmission is detected early and contained before sustained human-to-human transmission can emerge,” Dr Kole stated. Laboratory-Confirmed Human H5N1 Infections Referring to the two previous H5N1 infections in India, Dr Kole said, “India has recorded three laboratory-confirmed human H5N1 infections since 2003, and all three have been fatal. The first was a child in Haryana in 2021 who developed severe respiratory illness and Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome (ARDS) and died, while two further fatal human infections were reported in 2025. Although the number of cases remains extremely small, the high severity of documented H5N1 infection is a serious concern.” Globally, WHO has reported hundreds of confirmed human infections with a historically high case-fatality proportion, although this should not be interpreted as the expected fatality rate in a future widespread outbreak, he said. “Importantly, there is currently no evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission, and the overall public-health risk remains low. The concern is therefore not that India is facing an H5N1 pandemic today, but that every human infection provides an opportunity for the virus to adapt or acquire mutations that could alter its transmissibility; the three fatal cases should consequently be regarded as a strong surveillance and preparedness signal, not a reason for public panic,” added Dr Kole. At the WHO regional meeting, India showcased how integrating influenza surveillance with the Integrated Health Information Platform (IHIP) provides near-real-time data for analysis and visualisation, supporting faster detection of influenza trends and outbreaks. As India deals with H1N1 while continuing to monitor H5N1 outbreaks in birds, experts say surveillance systems must ensure that an unusual influenza signal is rapidly identified, investigated and acted upon. Concern For India For India, the concern is not merely theoretical.
Location and population
India, with specific mention of Kerala and New Delhi.
Event
WHO has issued an alert about the H5N1 virus, which is gaining urgency due to India's simultaneous battle with seasonal influenza A(H1N1) and avian influenza outbreaks. The alert is based on the ongoing spread of H5N1 among birds and the potential for animal-to-human spillover.
Scale, impact and risk
Avian influenza outbreaks have been reported in poultry and other birds in the past, with 11 H5N1 outbreaks in Kerala involving the deaths of over 54,000 birds and thousands more culled as a precaution. According to WHO data, between 2020-2024, India reported one death, and two deaths in 2025, due to avian influenza A(H5N1). No confirmed human H5N1 case has been reported in India so far in 2026. The risk of H5N1 to the general population remains low, but sustained surveillance is essential.
Response, status and uncertainty
The WHO has called for stronger One Health surveillance to detect zoonotic threats early. Public health experts emphasize the importance of laboratory networks and early-warning systems. There is currently no evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission of H5N1, and the overall public-health risk remains low.

德里学校要求加强针对 季节性流感、H1N1 流感的预防措施 - ET HealthWorld

Delhi schools asked to strengthen preventive measures against seasonal influenza, H1N1 - ET HealthWorld
新闻简报德里政府已发布建议,敦促所有公立和私立学校加强针对 季节性流感(包括 H1N1 流感)的预防措施,作为“健康学校-健康社区”倡议的一部分。该通报于 2026 年 8 月 26 日上午 11:45(印度标准时间)发布,强调德里或该国不存在紧急情况,无需恐慌。教育局建议采取呼吸道卫生、洗手、与有呼吸道症状的人保持距离以及必要时使用口罩等措施。还建议学校定期清洁教室和公共区域,确保适当通风,并养成饮水和休息等健康习惯。地区和地区教育副主任的任务是监督合规情况,并在 8 月 31 日之前提交一份汇编报告。该通报没有报告具体病例数、死亡或暴露情况,但强调了提高认识和采取负责任的预防行动以降低学校环境中感染风险的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
德里政府于 2026 年 8 月 26 日上午 11:45(印度标准时间)发布了该建议。该通报于同一日期和时间发布。
地点与对象
该建议适用于德里所有公立和私立学校,针对的是该市教育机构内的学生和工作人员。
事件
作为“健康学校-健康社区”倡议的一部分,建议德里学校加强针对 季节性流感(包括 H1N1 流感)的预防措施。该通报由教育局发布,强调不要出现恐慌或紧急情况。
规模、影响与风险
该建议强调了学校提高认识和预防措施对于减缓 季节性流感 和 H1N1 传播的重要性。它没有报告具体的病例数、死亡人数或暴露情况,但强调需要做好准备和及时采取行动。
应对、状态与不确定性
德里政府已指示学校采取预防措施,例如呼吸道卫生、勤洗手、与有症状的人保持距离以及必要时使用口罩。地区和地区教育副主任正在监督合规情况,并于 8 月 31 日之前提交报告。该通报并未表明任何已确认的 暴发 或紧急情况。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Delhi schools asked to strengthen preventive measures against seasonal influenza, H1N1 - ET HealthWorld

News Brief
The Delhi government has issued an advisory urging all government and private schools to enhance preventive measures against seasonal influenza, including H1N1, as part of a "Healthy School-Healthy Community" initiative. The advisory, published on August 26, 2026, at 11:45 AM IST, emphasizes that there is no emergency situation in Delhi or the country, and no need for panic. The Directorate of Education has recommended practices such as respiratory hygiene, handwashing, maintaining distance from individuals with respiratory symptoms, and using masks when necessary. Schools are also advised to clean classrooms and common areas regularly, ensure proper ventilation, and promote healthy habits like hydration and rest. District and zonal deputy directors of education are tasked with monitoring compliance and submitting a compiled report by August 31. The advisory does not report specific case counts, deaths, or exposures but underscores the importance of awareness and responsible preventive actions to reduce the risk of infection in the school environment.
Original source text (English)

These ar ETHealth Newsletters Explore and Subscribe to our Daily Newsletters Policy 2 min read Delhi schools asked to strengthen preventive measures against seasonal influenza, H1N1 The Directorate of Education advised schools to adopt a "Healthy School-Healthy Community" approach and reinforce respiratory hygiene and other preventive practices among students and staff. PTI Published On Aug 26, 2026 at 11:45 AM IST Select ETHealthworld as Preferred Choice on Google Copy Link Share on WhatsApp Share on Linkedin Share on X Share on Telegram Share on Facebook New Delhi: The Delhi government has asked all government and private schools to strengthen awareness and preventive measures against seasonal influenza, including H1N1, while stressing that there is no need for panic. The Directorate of Education advised schools to adopt a "Healthy School-Healthy Community" approach and reinforce respiratory hygiene and other preventive practices among students and staff. "There is no need for panic. There is no emergency situation in Delhi or in the country. Seasonal illnesses, including influenza, are seen during this period of the year. Our focus is on preparedness, awareness and timely preventive action," Delhi Education Minister Ashish Sood said. Advt "I urge citizens to remain calm, vigilant and follow the health advisory issued by the authorities," he added. According to the advisory, symptoms such as sore throat, cough, difficulty in breathing, body ache, headache, fatigue, chills and fever may be associated with seasonal influenza or H1N1. It said timely awareness and responsible preventive practices were important, particularly in the school environment. Schools have been advised to encourage students and staff to cover their nose and mouth while coughing or sneezing and dispose of tissues properly. Regular handwashing with soap and water, particularly before eating and after coughing or sneezing, has also been recommended. The schools have been asked to maintain appropriate distance from people showing respiratory symptoms, with masks to be used where considered necessary. They have also been advised to regularly clean classrooms, common areas and frequently touched surfaces and ensure adequate ventilation wherever feasible, it stated. The advisory also asked schools to generate awareness against spitting on school premises and in public places and encourage healthy habits, including adequate hydration, nutritious food, sufficient rest and personal hygiene. According to the DoE, students and staff have been advised to avoid frequently touching their eyes, nose and mouth to reduce the risk of infection. Schools have also been told to discourage self-medication, with antibiotics and other medicines not to be taken without medical advice. Advt It asked schools to spread awareness beyond classrooms through morning assemblies, classroom interactions, notice boards, parent communication channels and digital platforms. District and zonal deputy directors of education have been directed to monitor compliance and ensure that the measures are followed. They have been asked to submit a compiled compliance report to the Directorate by August 31, it added. Location and population
The advisory applies to all government and private schools in Delhi, targeting students and staff within the city's educational institutions.
Event
Delhi schools were advised to strengthen preventive measures against seasonal influenza, including H1N1, as part of a "Healthy School-Healthy Community" initiative. The advisory was issued by the Directorate of Education and emphasized no panic or emergency situation.
Scale, impact and risk
The advisory highlights the importance of awareness and preventive practices in schools to mitigate the spread of seasonal influenza and H1N1. It does not report specific case counts, deaths, or exposures but stresses the need for preparedness and timely action.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Delhi government has directed schools to implement preventive measures such as respiratory hygiene, handwashing, maintaining distance from symptomatic individuals, and using masks when necessary. Compliance is being monitored by district and zonal deputy directors of education, with a report due by August 31. The advisory does not indicate any confirmed outbreak or emergency situation.

Jharkhand on alert over seasonal influenza; hospitals to set up isolation wards - The Jharkhand Story

News Brief
Jharkhand has been placed on alert due to seasonal influenza, prompting hospitals to prepare isolation wards. The alert was reported on August 31, 2026, by The Jharkhand Story. The situation is under investigation, and no specific case counts or risk levels have been provided in the evidence. The response includes setting up isolation wards to manage potential outbreaks. The alert is part of broader public health preparedness efforts in the region. The Jharkhand Story is a local publisher, and the report is based on current developments. No further details on the scale of the outbreak or specific risk assessments were included in the supplied evidence.

泰米尔纳德邦季节性流感疫情受控:卫生部 - 《印度教》

Seasonal influenza under control in Tamil Nadu: Health Department - The Hindu
新闻简报印度泰米尔纳德邦卫生部于2026年8月26日报告称,尽管在季风季节出现病例,季节性流感疫情形势仍保持受控。公共卫生与预防医学局正在监测暴发疫情,并建议公众如出现症状应及时就医,并采取预防措施,如勤洗手和保持呼吸道卫生。未提供具体病例或死亡人数,但卫生部强调疫情形势稳定。该报告于2026年8月27日发布,强调在管理暴发疫情方面的持续努力,但未显示任何重大的卫生系统或运行影响。信息基于官方新闻稿和公共卫生建议。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该报告发布于2026年8月27日,事件日期为2026年8月26日。
地点与对象
受影响人群为泰米尔纳德邦公众。
事件
泰米尔纳德邦卫生部表示,季节性流感疫情形势仍保持受控,季风季节出现病例。公共卫生与预防医学局正在监测疫情,并建议公众如出现症状应及时就医,并采取预防措施,如勤洗手和保持呼吸道卫生。
规模、影响与风险
报告提及季风季节出现季节性流感病例,但未提供具体病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。卫生部明确表示疫情受控,显示未报告重大卫生系统或运行影响。
应对、状态与不确定性
公共卫生与预防医学局正密切监测疫情。卫生部已发布新闻稿并向公众发布预防措施建议。未提及信息来源存在未解决的问题或局限性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Seasonal influenza under control in Tamil Nadu: Health Department - The Hindu

News Brief
The Health Department of Tamil Nadu, India, reported on August 26, 2026, that the seasonal influenza situation remains under control despite cases being reported during the monsoon season. The Directorate of Public Health and Preventive Medicine is monitoring the outbreak, and the public has been advised to seek medical help if symptoms occur and to take precautionary measures such as frequent handwashing and maintaining respiratory hygiene. No specific case or death counts were provided, but the department emphasized that the situation is stable. The report, published on August 27, 2026, highlights the ongoing efforts to manage the outbreak without indicating any significant health-system or operational impacts. The information is based on an official press release and public health advisories.
Original source text (English)

H1N1预防:流感疫苗真的每个季节都有效吗?

H1N1 prevention: Is the influenza vaccine actually effective every season?
新闻简报Anurag Aggarwal 博士解释了为什么需要预防 H1N1 病毒。以下是季节性流感 疫苗 在降低风险方面的效果以及为什么需要预防。

时间
发布日期:2026-08-27;文章没有具体说明活动日期。

德里 H1N1 病例上升,但专家没有发现新的危险毒株的迹象 - 印度时报

H1N1 cases rise in Delhi, but experts see no sign of new dangerous strain - The Times of India
新闻简报在印度德里,本季度甲型H1N1流感病例急剧增加,该病毒占甲型流感病例的最大比例。增加的原因是潮湿的天气、室内拥挤和通风不良等因素,而不是新的或异常危险的菌株。印度医学研究委员会 (ICMR) 表示,正在传播的 H1N1 病毒正在出现预期的季节性变化,并且没有证据表明存在新的危险变种。卫生部长回顾了这一情况,强调病例上升幅度很大,但与更严重的疫情无关。公共卫生指导包括对有流感样症状的人呆在家里、戴口罩和洗手。建议每年流感 疫苗接种,特别是对于高危人群,如儿童、老年人和孕妇。当前的 传播 被认为是季节性的,没有迹象表明 传播 模式异常或严重性增加。

时间
该报告于 2026 年 8 月 29 日发布。没有明确说明事件日期,但指出 H1N1 病例的增加发生在当前季节。
地点与对象
据报道,该事件发生在印度德里。受影响的人群包括有流感样症状的人,特别是儿童、老年人和孕妇,他们的风险较高。
事件
本季,德里的 H1N1 病例急剧增加,该病毒占甲型流感病例的最大比例。专家表示,这种增加似乎与新的或异常危险的毒株无关,并且正在传播的病毒显示出与正常 季节性流感 一致的变化。
规模、影响与风险
截至周四,德里报告了 2,612 例 H1N1 病例,甲型流感病例总数超过 3,300 例。增加的原因包括天气潮湿、室内拥挤和通风不良等因素。卫生部长已经审查了情况,没有证据表明存在新的危险菌株。该风险被认为是季节性的,不会因异常因素而加剧。

印度医学研究委员会 (ICMR) 已确认,正在传播的 H1N1 病毒正在出现预期的季节性变化。卫生部长召开会议审查猪流感情况。没有具体证据表明国际旅行正在推动增长,当地和季节性因素被认为更为重要。该应对措施包括针对有流感样症状的个人的公共卫生指导。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

H1N1 cases rise in Delhi, but experts see no sign of new dangerous strain - The Times of India

News Brief
In Delhi, India, H1N1 cases have risen sharply during the current season, with the virus accounting for the largest share of influenza A cases. The increase is attributed to factors such as humid weather, indoor crowding, and poor ventilation, rather than a new or unusually dangerous strain. According to the Indian Council of Medical Research (ICMR), the circulating H1N1 virus is showing expected seasonal changes, and there is no evidence of a new dangerous variant. The health minister has reviewed the situation, emphasizing that the rise is significant but not linked to a more severe strain. Public health guidance includes staying home, wearing masks, and washing hands for individuals with influenza-like symptoms. Annual influenza vaccination is recommended, especially for those at higher risk, such as children, older adults, and pregnant women. The current outbreak is considered seasonal, with no indication of unusual transmission patterns or increased severity.

However, the increase does not appear to be linked to a new or unusually dangerous strain, with the circulating virus showing changes broadly consistent with normal seasonal influenza.According to the information in the graphic, Delhi had recorded 2,612 H1N1 cases by Thursday, while the number of influenza A cases had crossed 3,300. Watch H1N1 Cases Rise In Delhi: Health Minister Nadda Holds Meet, Reviews Swine Flu Situation Weather, crowding behind riseThe increase has been linked to a combination of humid, fluctuating and rainy weather, indoor crowding and poor ventilation. Increased person-to-person contact is also contributing to transmission, while H3N2 and other respiratory viruses continue to circulate.The Indian Council of Medical Research (ICMR) has said the circulating H1N1 virus is showing expected seasonal changes. The lineage has been circulating in India since 2025, and genetic changes observed so far remain consistent with seasonal influenza. There is no evidence of a new or unusually dangerous strain.There is also no specific evidence that international travel is driving the current rise. Local and seasonal factors such as weather, crowding and close contact appear to be more important. by Taboolaby Taboola Sponsored LinksSponsored Links Promoted LinksPromoted Links You May Like This "Snorkel Alternative" Aids Sleep for A Fraction of the CostModern Wellness Learn More Undo Symptoms can overlap with other infectionsCommon H1N1 symptoms include sudden fever, cough and sore throat, headache, body ache, fatigue and weakness. Children may also experience vomiting or diarrhoea.Doctors caution that these symptoms can overlap with H3N2 and other respiratory viruses, dengue, chikungunya and malaria. Symptoms alone, therefore, cannot confirm an H1N1 infection.Who needs greater caution?Older adults, young children and pregnant women are among those at higher risk. People with diabetes, asthma, COPD, chronic heart or lung disease, or weakened immunity also need to be particularly careful.H1N1 and Covid-19 are caused by different viruses. The Covid vaccine does not protect against H1N1, which has a separate influenza vaccine. Combined mRNA vaccines covering influenza and Covid are not commercially available.H1N1 is a subtype of influenza A, while H3N2 is another influenza A subtype. Influenza A and B can produce similar symptoms, with severity depending more on an individual's risk factors and complications than on the H1N1 label alone. Watch for warning signs While most influenza infections can be managed with appropriate care, worsening symptoms warrant medical attention. Breathlessness, falling oxygen levels, chest tightness or discomfort, persistent or worsening cough or fever, confusion, dehydration, or symptoms worsening after an initial improvement are warning signs. What people can do People with influenza-like symptoms should stay home, wear a mask, cover coughs and sneezes and wash their hands regularly. Improving indoor ventilation and avoiding close contact while ill can help limit transmission.Adequate rest and hydration are also important. Annual influenza vaccination should be considered, particularly by people at higher risk.The message for Delhiites is one of caution rather than panic: the current rise is significant, but the available information does not point to a new or unusually dangerous H1N1 strain. 日本流感季节提前一个月开始,为 1999 年以来第二快 - Streamlinefeed.co.ke

Japan's Flu Season Starts a Month Early, Second Fastest Since 1999 - streamlinefeed.co.ke
新闻简报据厚生劳动省称,日本流感季节于 2026 年 8 月 31 日提前一个月开始,这是自 1999 年以来第二早开始的流感季节。由快速传播的 H3N2 分支 K 驱动的 暴发 在冲绳县的病例负担很高,每个机构有 12.18 例病例,其次是东京和宫崎县。尽管症状并不比以前的菌株更严重,但早期发病引起了人们对传染性的担忧。政府已宣布全国范围内出现疫情,并敦促疫苗接种和公众保持警惕。学校和医院已经采取了关闭和加强感染预防等措施。这些数据正在全球范围内受到监测,包括世界卫生组织,以便为 疫苗 策略和冬季准备工作提供信息。早期流感季节为北半球提供了一个不寻常的警告信号,因为 监测 流感通常从秋季持续到春季。
查看新闻五要素
时间
日本的流感季节提前一个月开始,即2026年8月31日,这是自1999年以来第二早开始的流感季节。活动日期为2026年8月31日,发布日期为2026年8月28日。
地点与对象
暴发 主要在日本报道,特别提到了冲绳县、东京和宫崎县。受影响的人群包括普通公众,重点是全国各地的医院和诊所。
事件
日本已正式进入流感季节,该季节比去年提前一个月开始,是自 1999 年开始全国追踪以来第二早发病的流感季节。厚生劳动省根据报告的超过流行阈值的病例确认了这一事件。
规模、影响与风险
冲绳县的案件负担最高,为每个机构 12.18 件,其次是东京(3.3 件)和宫崎县(2.89 件)。暴发 被描述为快速传播的 H3N2 亚支 K,关注的是传播性而不是严重性。暴发 的规模非常巨大,早期数据已输入全球健康监测系统。
应对、状态与不确定性
厚生劳动省已宣布全国流感流行,并敦促 疫苗接种 和公众保持警惕。学校已报告关闭,医院加强了感染预防措施。应对工作正在进行中,国际社会正在对 暴发 及其对 疫苗 规划的影响进行监测。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Japan's Flu Season Starts a Month Early, Second Fastest Since 1999 - streamlinefeed.co.ke

News Brief
Japan's flu season began a month early on August 31, 2026, marking the second-earliest start since 1999, according to the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare. The outbreak, driven by a fast-spreading H3N2 subclade K, has seen high case burdens in Okinawa Prefecture, with 12.18 cases per institution, followed by Tokyo and Miyazaki Prefecture. The early onset raises concerns about transmissibility, though symptoms do not appear more severe than previous strains. The government has declared a nationwide epidemic and is urging vaccination and public vigilance. Schools and hospitals have already taken measures, such as closures and enhanced infection prevention, in response. This data is being monitored globally, including by the World Health Organization, to inform vaccine strategies and winter preparedness. The early season provides an unusual warning signal for the northern hemisphere, as influenza surveillance typically runs from autumn through spring.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Global editionMonday, August 31, 2026 Streamline Search⌘KWriteSign inJoin Streamline StreamlineGlobal edition LatestWorldBusinessPoliticsTechnologyScienceCultureSportDirectoryHouse HuntingPeopleHistoryMore LatestWorldBusinessPoliticsTechnologyScienceCultureSport Back to News/Health Health Japan's Flu Season Starts a Month Early, Second Fastest Since 1999 Japan has entered flu season a month early, its second-earliest start since 1999, with Okinawa at 12.18 cases per institution and experts eyeing a fast-spreading H3N2 subclade K. The first 10 seconds cannot be skipped. Ad playing Ad playing. Learn more Build your reading streak0/3 today·Counts at 25% Feverish patients crowded paediatric wards a full month before Tokyo's hospitals usually brace for winter, and on Friday the confirmation came from above: Japan has officially entered its influenza season, roughly a month ahead of last year and the second-earliest start recorded since national tracking began in 1999, according to the country's health ministry. The declaration by the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare matters far beyond Japan's borders. Influenza surveillance in temperate countries typically runs from autumn through spring, so an August-scale surge in a nation of more than 120 million people hands global health agencies an unusually early warning signal for the northern hemisphere winter, and virologists are already asking whether the virus is spreading faster than it did last season. What the surveillance data shows Japan does not declare a flu season casually. The Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare relies on a sentinel network of thousands of hospitals and clinics nationwide, and the season is officially declared once reported cases cross the epidemic threshold, an average of at least one patient per reporting institution per week. Passing that mark so early in the calendar is rare: since comparable records began in 1999, only one season has started sooner. The geographic spread is uneven but broad. According to ministry surveillance figures reported by Xinhua, Okinawa Prefecture recorded the highest case burden, with an average of 12.18 cases per institution, followed by Tokyo at 3.3 and Miyazaki Prefecture at 2.89, all well above the epidemic threshold. Public health tracking compiled by regional observers lists Okinawa, Tokyo and Hokkaido among the hardest-hit areas. Epidemic threshold for a national declaration: an average of 1.0 or more influenza patients per sentinel institution per week, per Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare methodology. Highest prefecture burden: Okinawa, at 12.18 cases per institution. Tokyo: 3.3 cases per institution; Miyazaki: 2.89. Timing: roughly one month earlier than last season's start and the second-earliest onset since surveillance records began in 1999. A variant under the microscope The speed of the surge has focused attention on the strain driving it. Virologists point to a subclade of influenza A(H3N2), informally labelled subclade K, as a possible explanation for the higher case numbers. Importantly, early assessment suggests the variant does not appear to cause worse symptoms than circulating strains; the concern is transmissibility, not severity, according to analysis of the variant published by virology commentators monitoring the season. That distinction matters for vaccine planners. If subclade K has drifted far enough from the strains used to formulate this season's vaccines, protection could be weaker even though the jabs still reduce the risk of severe disease. International monitors, including the World Health Organization's Global Influenza Surveillance and Response System, continuously sequence circulating viruses to test whether the current vaccine match holds, and Japan's early data will feed directly into that global picture. Why is Japan being hit so early? Health officials in Tokyo have suggested the epidemic may be driven by several compounding factors rather than a single cause. One prominent hypothesis reported by TIME is the return of mass tourism, which has restored international passenger volumes to near pre-pandemic levels and, with them, new opportunities for respiratory viruses to circulate and seed clusters across the archipelago. History raises the stakes. Last season, Japan endured what researchers described as its largest influenza outbreak in 25 years, with more than 317,000 cases reported in a single week between December 23 and 29. An early start this season, arriving before population immunity from that wave has fully consolidated, gives the virus a longer runway. Some experts have gone further, warning publicly that the virus may be evolving and spreading faster than it did last year, a concern amplified by international broadcasters covering the outbreak. The response, and what it means for the rest of the world The government has declared a nationwide flu epidemic and is urging the public to get vaccinated and remain vigilant, according to reports on the official response. The practical consequences are already visible: schools have reported closures as clusters spread through classrooms, and hospital bodies have been urged to strengthen infection prevention and control measures nearly five weeks ahead of the usual timetable, according to health-security monitoring outlets. For readers outside Japan, the early season reads differently depending on where you live. In the United Kingdom, the National Health Service and the UK Health Security Agency use southern hemisphere and east Asian surveillance data to tune winter preparedness, from vaccine uptake campaigns to hospital capacity planning. In Australia, which sits in the southern hemisphere's influenza belt, epidemiologists watch Japanese sequencing data for early clues about dominant strains. And in East Africa, where Kenya's Ministry of Health tracks influenza through its national surveillance system with support from research institutions such as KEMRI, the Japanese data informs strain selection for vaccines available in the region. The message from Tokyo's early surge is consistent with what influenza researchers have repeated for years: flu seasons are growing harder to predict, and the countries that read the earliest signals most carefully tend to buy themselves the most preparation time. Japan's sentinel data, refreshed weekly, will now be among the most closely watched datasets in global public health as the northern winter approaches. How did this story land? No sign-in required. View five news elements
Time
Japan's flu season started a month early, on August 31, 2026, marking the second-earliest start since 1999. The event date is August 31, 2026, with the publication date being August 28, 2026.
Location and population
The outbreak is primarily reported in Japan, with specific mention of Okinawa Prefecture, Tokyo, and Miyazaki Prefecture. The affected population includes the general public, with a focus on hospitals and clinics across the country.
Event
Japan has officially entered its influenza season, which started a month earlier than last year and is the second-earliest onset since national tracking began in 1999. The event is confirmed by the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare based on reported cases crossing the epidemic threshold.
Scale, impact and risk
Okinawa Prefecture recorded the highest case burden at 12.18 cases per institution, followed by Tokyo at 3.3 and Miyazaki Prefecture at 2.89. The outbreak is described as a fast-spreading H3N2 subclade K, with concerns about transmissibility rather than severity. The scale of the outbreak is significant, with early data feeding into global health monitoring systems.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare has declared a nationwide flu epidemic and is urging vaccination and public vigilance. Schools have reported closures, and hospitals have strengthened infection prevention measures. The response is ongoing, with international monitoring of the outbreak and its implications for vaccine planning.
